===== FILE: src/core/engine/geo.ts =====
// Project geometry: coordinates, alignments, distances, and KML/GeoJSON import/export. No map library here.
import type { AppState, Project } from '../types'

export type LatLng = [number, number]

/** Demo utility footprint: the schematic map (x 0–1000, y 0–600) is laid over this box. */
export const DEMO_BOUNDS = { north: 36.25, south: 34.95, west: -121.0, east: -118.95 }
export function xyToLatLng(x: number, y: number): LatLng {
  const b = DEMO_BOUNDS
  return [+(b.north - (y / 600) * (b.north - b.south)).toFixed(6), +(b.west + (x / 1000) * (b.east - b.west)).toFixed(6)]
}

const R_FT = 20902231 // earth radius in feet
const rad = (d: number) => (d * Math.PI) / 180
export function distanceFt(a: LatLng, b: LatLng): number {
  const dLat = rad(b[0] - a[0]), dLng = rad(b[1] - a[1])
  const h = Math.sin(dLat / 2) ** 2 + Math.cos(rad(a[0])) * Math.cos(rad(b[0])) * Math.sin(dLng / 2) ** 2
  return 2 * R_FT * Math.asin(Math.min(1, Math.sqrt(h)))
}
export function lengthFt(route: LatLng[]): number {
  let t = 0
  for (let i = 1; i < route.length; i++) t += distanceFt(route[i - 1], route[i])
  return Math.round(t)
}
/** Move from a point by a distance (ft) on a bearing (degrees from north). */
export function offset(p: LatLng, distFt: number, bearingDeg: number): LatLng {
  const d = distFt / R_FT, br = rad(bearingDeg), la = rad(p[0]), lo = rad(p[1])
  const la2 = Math.asin(Math.sin(la) * Math.cos(d) + Math.cos(la) * Math.sin(d) * Math.cos(br))
  const lo2 = lo + Math.atan2(Math.sin(br) * Math.sin(d) * Math.cos(la), Math.cos(d) - Math.sin(la) * Math.sin(la2))
  return [+((la2 * 180) / Math.PI).toFixed(6), +((lo2 * 180) / Math.PI).toFixed(6)]
}

/** Shortest distance (ft) from a point to a polyline, with the nearest point. */
export function distanceToLineFt(p: LatLng, line: LatLng[]): { ft: number; at: LatLng } {
  let best = { ft: Infinity, at: line[0] ?? p }
  const k = Math.cos(rad(p[0]))
  const xy = (q: LatLng) => [q[1] * k, q[0]] as const
  const P = xy(p)
  for (let i = 1; i < line.length; i++) {
    const A = xy(line[i - 1]), B = xy(line[i])
    const dx = B[0] - A[0], dy = B[1] - A[1]
    const t = dx || dy ? Math.max(0, Math.min(1, ((P[0] - A[0]) * dx + (P[1] - A[1]) * dy) / (dx * dx + dy * dy))) : 0
    const at: LatLng = [line[i - 1][0] + t * (line[i][0] - line[i - 1][0]), line[i - 1][1] + t * (line[i][1] - line[i - 1][1])]
    const ft = distanceFt(p, at)
    if (ft < best.ft) best = { ft, at }
  }
  if (line.length === 1) best = { ft: distanceFt(p, line[0]), at: line[0] }
  return best
}

/** Minimum distance between two polylines (vertex-to-segment both ways; good enough at project scale). */
export function lineToLineFt(a: LatLng[], b: LatLng[]): number {
  let m = Infinity
  for (const p of a) m = Math.min(m, distanceToLineFt(p, b).ft)
  for (const p of b) m = Math.min(m, distanceToLineFt(p, a).ft)
  return m
}

export function projectPoint(p: Project): LatLng | undefined {
  if (p.location.lat != null && p.location.lng != null) return [p.location.lat, p.location.lng]
  const r = p.location.route
  if (r && r.length) return r[Math.floor(r.length / 2)]
  return undefined
}
export function projectBounds(projects: Project[]): [LatLng, LatLng] | undefined {
  const pts = projects.flatMap((p) => [...(p.location.route ?? []), ...(projectPoint(p) ? [projectPoint(p)!] : [])])
  if (!pts.length) return undefined
  return [[Math.min(...pts.map((x) => x[0])), Math.min(...pts.map((x) => x[1]))], [Math.max(...pts.map((x) => x[0])), Math.max(...pts.map((x) => x[1]))]]
}

export const googleMapsUrl = (p: LatLng) => `https://www.google.com/maps/search/?api=1&query=${p[0]},${p[1]}`
export const googleEarthUrl = (p: LatLng) => `https://earth.google.com/web/@${p[0]},${p[1]},300a,1500d,35y,0h,0t,0r`
export const fmtLatLng = (p?: LatLng) => (p ? `${p[0].toFixed(5)}, ${p[1].toFixed(5)}` : '—')

/**
 * Parse coordinates typed or pasted by a person. Accepts "lat, lng" per line, "lat lng", a Google Maps
 * URL (…/@34.05,-118.24,… or ?q=34.05,-118.24), or lng,lat pairs (detected when the first value is
 * outside ±90). Returns [] when nothing valid is found.
 */
export function parseCoordinates(text: string): LatLng[] {
  const out: LatLng[] = []
  const url = text.match(/[@=](-?\d{1,2}\.\d+),\s*(-?\d{1,3}\.\d+)/)
  if (url && !text.includes('\n')) return [[+url[1], +url[2]]]
  for (const raw of text.split(/[\n;]+/)) {
    const nums = raw.match(/-?\d+(?:\.\d+)?/g)
    if (!nums || nums.length < 2) continue
    let a = +nums[0], b = +nums[1]
    if (Math.abs(a) > 90 && Math.abs(b) <= 90) [a, b] = [b, a]
    if (Math.abs(a) <= 90 && Math.abs(b) <= 180) out.push([a, b])
  }
  return out
}

/** Read Points and LineStrings from a KML document (Google Earth export). */
export function parseKML(text: string): { points: LatLng[]; lines: LatLng[][] } {
  const doc = new DOMParser().parseFromString(text, 'application/xml')
  const read = (el: Element) => (el.textContent ?? '').trim().split(/\s+/).map((t) => t.split(',').map(Number)).filter((c) => c.length >= 2 && !isNaN(c[0]) && !isNaN(c[1])).map((c) => [c[1], c[0]] as LatLng)
  const lines = Array.from(doc.getElementsByTagName('LineString')).map((ls) => read(ls.getElementsByTagName('coordinates')[0])).filter((l) => l.length > 1)
  const points = Array.from(doc.getElementsByTagName('Point')).map((pt) => read(pt.getElementsByTagName('coordinates')[0])[0]).filter(Boolean)
  return { points, lines }
}

/** Read Points and (Multi)LineStrings from GeoJSON (Esri / QGIS export). */
export function parseGeoJSON(obj: any): { points: LatLng[]; lines: LatLng[][] } {
  const points: LatLng[] = [], lines: LatLng[][] = []
  const geom = (g: any) => {
    if (!g) return
    if (g.type === 'Point') points.push([g.coordinates[1], g.coordinates[0]])
    if (g.type === 'LineString') lines.push(g.coordinates.map((c: number[]) => [c[1], c[0]]))
    if (g.type === 'MultiLineString') g.coordinates.forEach((l: number[][]) => lines.push(l.map((c) => [c[1], c[0]])))
    if (g.type === 'GeometryCollection') g.geometries.forEach(geom)
  }
  if (obj?.type === 'FeatureCollection') obj.features.forEach((f: any) => geom(f.geometry))
  else if (obj?.type === 'Feature') geom(obj.geometry)
  else geom(obj)
  return { points, lines }
}

export function projectsToGeoJSON(s: AppState, projects: Project[]) {
  return {
    type: 'FeatureCollection',
    features: projects.flatMap((p) => {
      const props = { code: p.code, name: p.name, program: s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)?.code, stage: p.stageIndex + 1, status: p.status, lineId: p.asset.lineId, diameterIn: p.asset.diameterIn, maopPsig: p.asset.maopPsig }
      const out: any[] = []
      const pt = projectPoint(p)
      if (pt) out.push({ type: 'Feature', properties: { ...props, geometry: 'site' }, geometry: { type: 'Point', coordinates: [pt[1], pt[0]] } })
      if (p.location.route?.length) out.push({ type: 'Feature', properties: { ...props, geometry: 'alignment', lengthFt: lengthFt(p.location.route) }, geometry: { type: 'LineString', coordinates: p.location.route.map((c) => [c[1], c[0]]) } })
      return out
    }),
  }
}

export function projectsToKML(projects: Project[], title: string): string {
  const esc = (t: string) => t.replace(/&/g, '&amp;').replace(/</g, '&lt;')
  const pm = projects.flatMap((p) => {
    const pt = projectPoint(p)
    const parts: string[] = []
    if (pt) parts.push(`<Placemark><name>${esc(p.code)}</name><description>${esc(p.name)}</description><Point><coordinates>${pt[1]},${pt[0]},0</coordinates></Point></Placemark>`)
    if (p.location.route?.length) parts.push(`<Placemark><name>${esc(p.code)} alignment</name><Style><LineStyle><color>ff00b0f0</color><width>4</width></LineStyle></Style><LineString><coordinates>${p.location.route.map((c) => `${c[1]},${c[0]},0`).join(' ')}</coordinates></LineString></Placemark>`)
    return parts
  })
  return `<?xml version="1.0" encoding="UTF-8"?><kml xmlns="http://www.opengis.net/kml/2.2"><Document><name>${esc(title)}</name>${pm.join('')}</Document></kml>`
}

/** Demo network lines within `ft` of a project's geometry (proximity check for the demo layer). */
export function nearbyNetwork(s: AppState, p: Project, ft = 1500) {
  const geom: LatLng[] = p.location.route?.length ? p.location.route : projectPoint(p) ? [projectPoint(p)!] : []
  if (!geom.length) return []
  return s.settings.gis.demoNetwork
    .map((n) => ({ line: n, ft: Math.round(geom.length > 1 ? lineToLineFt(geom, n.coords) : distanceToLineFt(geom[0], n.coords).ft) }))
    .filter((x) => x.ft <= ft)
    .sort((a, b) => a.ft - b.ft)
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/info.ts =====
// Project Information record: values live on the project (bound fields) or in project.info.
import type { AppState, InfoFieldDef, Project } from '../types'
import { get, nowISO, uid } from '../util'

export function infoValue(p: Project, def: InfoFieldDef, s?: AppState): string {
  if (def.key === 'recovery' && s) return s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId)?.recovery ?? ''
  if (def.key === 'program' && s) { const pr = s.programs.find((x) => x.id === p.programId); return pr ? `${pr.code} · ${pr.name}` : '' }
  if (def.key === 'pm' && s) return s.people.find((x) => x.id === p.team.pm)?.name ?? ''
  if (def.key === 'sponsor' && s) return s.people.find((x) => x.id === p.team.sponsor)?.name ?? ''
  const v = def.bind ? get(p, def.bind) : p.info?.[def.key]
  if (v === undefined || v === null) return ''
  if (typeof v === 'boolean') return v ? 'Yes' : 'No'
  return String(v)
}

/** Write one field back to the project draft (coerces to the bound field's type). */
export function setInfoValue(p: Project, def: InfoFieldDef, value: string) {
  if (!def.bind) { p.info = { ...(p.info ?? {}), [def.key]: value }; return }
  const parts = def.bind.split('.')
  let o: any = p
  for (const k of parts.slice(0, -1)) o = o[k]
  const last = parts[parts.length - 1]
  const cur = o[last]
  o[last] = def.type === 'number' || def.type === 'money' ? (value.trim() === '' ? undefined : Number(value.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, '')) || 0) : typeof cur === 'number' ? Number(value.replace(/[^0-9.-]/g, '')) || 0 : typeof cur === 'boolean' ? value === 'Yes' || value === 'true' : value
}

export const infoFieldsFor = (s: AppState) => s.settings.infoFields

/** Required fields still empty for the project's current stage (counted as a gate blocker). */
export function missingInfo(s: AppState, p: Project, stageIndex = p.stageIndex): InfoFieldDef[] {
  return s.settings.infoFields.filter((f) => f.requiredAtStage !== undefined && f.requiredAtStage <= stageIndex && !f.readOnly && !infoValue(p, f, s).trim())
}

export function infoCompleteness(s: AppState, p: Project): { filled: number; total: number; pct: number } {
  const fields = s.settings.infoFields.filter((f) => !f.readOnly)
  const filled = fields.filter((f) => infoValue(p, f, s).trim()).length
  return { filled, total: fields.length, pct: fields.length ? Math.round((filled / fields.length) * 100) : 100 }
}

/** Apply edits and write a revision (who, when, field-level from → to). Returns the revision or null if nothing changed. */
export function reviseInfo(d: AppState, pid: string, edits: Record<string, string>, note: string, byId = d.currentUserId) {
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)
  if (!p) return null
  const changes: { key: string; label: string; from: string; to: string }[] = []
  for (const [key, to] of Object.entries(edits)) {
    const def = d.settings.infoFields.find((f) => f.key === key)
    if (!def || def.readOnly) continue
    const from = infoValue(p, def, d)
    if (from === to) continue
    setInfoValue(p, def, to)
    changes.push({ key, label: def.label, from, to })
  }
  if (!changes.length) return null
  const rev = { id: uid('irev'), projectId: pid, at: nowISO(), byId, note, changes }
  d.infoRevisions.push(rev)
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: rev.at, actorId: byId, entity: 'project-info', entityId: pid, projectId: pid, action: 'revise', summary: `Project information: ${changes.map((c) => c.label).join(', ')}${note ? ` — ${note}` : ''}` })
  return rev
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/lines.ts =====
// Forecast line items under the WBS functions: budget, actuals and ETC by line, roll-ups to function,
// and per-function curve profiles. Pure functions; no storage or UI.
import type { AppState, CostPeriod, FnProfile, Forecast, ForecastLine, Money, Project, SpreadProfile } from '../types'
import { argmax, monthRange, sum } from '../util'
import { CONT_CAT, ESC_CAT, LOADER_CAT } from './estimates'
import { activeBudget } from './position'
import { curve, forecastMonths, SPREAD_METHODS, type SpreadMethod } from './wbs'

export type Grid = Record<string, Record<string, Money>> // key → month → $

export const forecastLines = (s: AppState): ForecastLine[] => s.settings.forecastLines.filter((l) => l.active)
export const linesOfFunction = (s: AppState, fnId: string) => forecastLines(s).filter((l) => l.functionId === fnId)
export const lineById = (s: AppState, id?: string) => s.settings.forecastLines.find((l) => l.id === id)
export const functionOfLine = (s: AppState, lineId: string) => lineById(s, lineId)?.functionId

/** Line a CBS category's budget belongs to (first active line that lists it). */
export const lineOfCategory = (s: AppState, categoryId: string) => forecastLines(s).find((l) => l.categoryIds.includes(categoryId))?.id

/** Split a by-category amount map into lines. Loaders and escalation go pro-rata to direct lines; contingency separate. */
export function toLines(s: AppState, byCategory: Record<string, Money>): { byLine: Record<string, Money>; contingency: Money; unmapped: Money } {
  const byLine: Record<string, Money> = Object.fromEntries(forecastLines(s).map((l) => [l.id, 0]))
  let pool = 0, contingency = 0, unmapped = 0
  for (const [cat, amt] of Object.entries(byCategory)) {
    if ((cat === LOADER_CAT || cat === ESC_CAT) && !lineOfCategory(s, cat)) { pool += amt; continue } // owned by a function (e.g. 900 Project Overhead) → direct; otherwise pro-rata
    if (cat === CONT_CAT) { contingency += amt; continue }
    const l = lineOfCategory(s, cat)
    if (l) byLine[l] += amt
    else unmapped += amt
  }
  return { byLine: allocate(byLine, pool), contingency, unmapped }
}

/** Add `pool` to a map pro-rata to its values (whole dollars, exact total). Even split when the base is zero. */
export function allocate(base: Record<string, Money>, pool: Money): Record<string, Money> {
  const out = { ...base }
  const ids = Object.keys(out)
  if (!pool || !ids.length) return out
  const tot = sum(Object.values(base))
  const w = ids.map((id) => (tot ? base[id] : 1))
  const adds = w.map((x) => Math.round((pool * x) / sum(w)))
  adds[argmax(w)] += pool - sum(adds)
  ids.forEach((id, i) => { out[id] += adds[i] })
  return out
}

/** Current budget by line (active budget + approved new-funding changes), contingency separate. */
export function budgetByLine(s: AppState, pid: string): { byLine: Record<string, Money>; contingency: Money } {
  const b = activeBudget(s, pid)
  const cats: Record<string, Money> = { ...(b?.byCategory ?? {}) }
  for (const c of s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid && c.status === 'approved' && c.fundedFrom === 'new-funding' && (!b || (c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt) >= b.createdAt))) cats[c.categoryId] = (cats[c.categoryId] || 0) + c.costImpact
  return { byLine: toLines(s, cats).byLine, contingency: b?.contingency ?? 0 }
}

/** Roll a line map up to functions. */
export function linesToFunctions(s: AppState, byLine: Record<string, Money>): Record<string, Money> {
  const out: Record<string, Money> = Object.fromEntries(s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((f) => [f.id, 0]))
  for (const [lid, v] of Object.entries(byLine)) { const fn = functionOfLine(s, lid); if (fn && fn in out) out[fn] += v }
  return out
}
/** Roll a line × month grid up to function × month. */
export function rollupGrid(s: AppState, lineGrid?: Grid): Grid {
  const out: Grid = Object.fromEntries(s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((f) => [f.id, {}]))
  for (const [lid, months] of Object.entries(lineGrid ?? {})) {
    const fn = functionOfLine(s, lid)
    if (!fn || !out[fn]) continue
    for (const [m, v] of Object.entries(months)) out[fn][m] = (out[fn][m] || 0) + v
  }
  return out
}

/** A period's actuals by line. Periods without a line split are allocated by line budget share. */
export function periodByLine(s: AppState, cp: CostPeriod, budget: Record<string, Money>): Record<string, Money> {
  if (cp.byLine) return cp.byLine
  if (cp.byFunction) {
    // split each function's actual to its lines by budget share
    const out: Record<string, Money> = {}
    for (const [fn, amt] of Object.entries(cp.byFunction)) {
      const ls = linesOfFunction(s, fn)
      if (!ls.length) continue
      Object.assign(out, allocate(Object.fromEntries(ls.map((l) => [l.id, budget[l.id] || 0])), amt))
    }
    return out
  }
  return allocate(Object.fromEntries(Object.keys(budget).map((k) => [k, budget[k]])), cp.actual)
}

export function actualsByLineMonth(s: AppState, pid: string): Grid {
  const out: Grid = Object.fromEntries(forecastLines(s).map((l) => [l.id, {}]))
  const bud = budgetByLine(s, pid).byLine
  for (const cp of s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid)) {
    for (const [lid, v] of Object.entries(periodByLine(s, cp, bud))) { out[lid] ??= {}; out[lid][cp.period] = (out[lid][cp.period] || 0) + v }
  }
  return out
}
export function actualsByLine(s: AppState, pid: string): Record<string, Money> {
  return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(actualsByLineMonth(s, pid)).map(([k, v]) => [k, sum(Object.values(v))]))
}

// ───────────────────────────── Profiles

export const BUILT_IN_PROFILES: { id: string; name: string; description: string }[] = SPREAD_METHODS.map((m) => ({ id: m.id, name: m.label, description: m.hint }))
export const isBuiltIn = (id: string) => SPREAD_METHODS.some((m) => m.id === id)
export function profileName(s: AppState, id: string) {
  return BUILT_IN_PROFILES.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? s.settings.spreadProfiles.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? id
}
export function allProfiles(s: AppState): { id: string; name: string; description: string; custom: boolean }[] {
  return [...BUILT_IN_PROFILES.map((p) => ({ ...p, custom: false })), ...s.settings.spreadProfiles.map((p) => ({ id: p.id, name: p.name, description: p.description, custom: true }))]
}

/** Resample a weight shape to n points (linear interpolation; area-preserving enough for phasing). */
export function resample(weights: number[], n: number): number[] {
  if (n <= 0) return []
  if (!weights.length) return Array(n).fill(1)
  if (weights.length === 1) return Array(n).fill(Math.max(0, weights[0]))
  return Array.from({ length: n }, (_, i) => {
    const t = n === 1 ? 0.5 : i / (n - 1)
    const x = t * (weights.length - 1)
    const a = Math.floor(x), b = Math.min(weights.length - 1, a + 1)
    return Math.max(0, weights[a] + (weights[b] - weights[a]) * (x - a))
  })
}

/** Weights for a profile over a list of months (honours black-out calendar months). */
export function profileWeights(s: AppState, profileId: string, months: string[]): number[] {
  const custom = s.settings.spreadProfiles.find((p) => p.id === profileId)
  let w = custom ? resample(custom.weights, months.length) : curve((isBuiltIn(profileId) ? profileId : 'even') as SpreadMethod, months.length)
  if (custom?.zeroMonths?.length) {
    const masked = w.map((x, i) => (custom.zeroMonths!.includes(Number(months[i].slice(5, 7))) ? 0 : x))
    if (sum(masked) > 0) w = masked
  }
  return w
}

/** Spread `total` over months by weights; whole dollars that sum exactly to total. */
export function spreadByWeights(total: Money, months: string[], w: number[]): Record<string, Money> {
  const W = sum(w) || 1
  const out: Record<string, Money> = {}
  let acc = 0
  const lastNz = w.reduce((li, x, i) => (x > 0 ? i : li), months.length - 1)
  months.forEach((m, i) => {
    const v = i === lastNz ? total - acc : Math.round((total * w[i]) / W)
    out[m] = v
    acc += v
  })
  return out
}

/**
 * Apply a profile to one function in a forecast: the function's ETC (`fnTotal`, default = its current ETC)
 * is split to its lines by each line's current ETC share (or remaining budget when the function is empty),
 * then each line is phased by the profile between start and end. Months outside the window are zeroed.
 * Returns the new line grid for that function's lines.
 */
export function applyProfile(s: AppState, p: Project, f: Pick<Forecast, 'lineSpread'>, fnId: string, prof: Pick<FnProfile, 'profileId' | 'start' | 'end'>, fnTotal?: Money): Grid {
  const lines = linesOfFunction(s, fnId)
  const cur = f.lineSpread ?? {}
  const curTot = Object.fromEntries(lines.map((l) => [l.id, sum(Object.values(cur[l.id] ?? {}))]))
  const total = fnTotal ?? sum(Object.values(curTot))
  let shares = curTot
  if (!sum(Object.values(shares))) {
    const bud = budgetByLine(s, p.id).byLine
    const act = actualsByLine(s, p.id)
    shares = Object.fromEntries(lines.map((l) => [l.id, Math.max(0, (bud[l.id] || 0) - (act[l.id] || 0))]))
  }
  const split = splitExact(total, Object.fromEntries(lines.map((l) => [l.id, shares[l.id] || 0])))
  const all = forecastMonths(s, p)
  const start = prof.start < all[0] ? all[0] : prof.start
  const end = prof.end < start ? start : prof.end
  const window = monthRange(start, end)
  const w = profileWeights(s, prof.profileId, window)
  const out: Grid = {}
  for (const l of lines) {
    const row: Record<string, Money> = Object.fromEntries(all.map((m) => [m, 0]))
    Object.assign(row, spreadByWeights(split[l.id] || 0, window, w))
    out[l.id] = row
  }
  return out
}

/** Split total across keys by weights (whole dollars, exact; even split when weights are all zero). */
export function splitExact(total: Money, weights: Record<string, number>): Record<string, Money> {
  const ids = Object.keys(weights)
  if (!ids.length) return {}
  const W = sum(Object.values(weights))
  const w = ids.map((id) => (W ? weights[id] : 1))
  const v = w.map((x) => Math.round((total * x) / sum(w)))
  v[argmax(w)] += total - sum(v)
  return Object.fromEntries(ids.map((id, i) => [id, v[i]]))
}

/** Default line spread for a new forecast: remaining budget per line (scaled to etcTotal), phased by the function's timing. */
export function defaultLineSpread(s: AppState, p: Project, etcTotal?: Money): { lineSpread: Grid; profiles: Record<string, FnProfile> } {
  const months = forecastMonths(s, p)
  const bud = budgetByLine(s, p.id).byLine
  const act = actualsByLine(s, p.id)
  const remaining = Object.fromEntries(forecastLines(s).map((l) => [l.id, Math.max(0, (bud[l.id] || 0) - (act[l.id] || 0))]))
  const target = etcTotal ?? sum(Object.values(remaining))
  const scaled = splitExact(target, remaining)
  const timing: Record<string, SpreadMethod> = { early: 'front', mid: 'scurve', late: 'back', flat: 'even' }
  const lineSpread: Grid = {}
  const profiles: Record<string, FnProfile> = {}
  for (const fn of s.settings.wbsFunctions) {
    const method = timing[fn.timing]
    profiles[fn.id] = { profileId: method, start: months[0], end: months[months.length - 1], adjusted: false }
    const w = profileWeights(s, method, months)
    for (const l of linesOfFunction(s, fn.id)) lineSpread[l.id] = spreadByWeights(Math.max(0, scaled[l.id] || 0), months, w)
  }
  return { lineSpread, profiles }
}

/**
 * Line grid for a forecast. Older forecast versions only carry `spread` by function: split each function's
 * months to its lines by remaining-budget share so every version can be read at line level.
 */
export function lineGridOf(s: AppState, p: Project, f?: Pick<Forecast, 'lineSpread' | 'spread'>): Grid {
  if (!f) return {}
  if (f.lineSpread) return f.lineSpread
  const bud = budgetByLine(s, p.id).byLine
  const act = actualsByLine(s, p.id)
  const out: Grid = {}
  for (const [fn, months] of Object.entries(f.spread ?? {})) {
    const ls = linesOfFunction(s, fn)
    const w = Object.fromEntries(ls.map((l) => [l.id, Math.max(0, (bud[l.id] || 0) - (act[l.id] || 0)) || 1]))
    for (const l of ls) out[l.id] = {}
    for (const [m, v] of Object.entries(months)) {
      const sp = splitExact(v, w)
      for (const l of ls) out[l.id][m] = sp[l.id]
    }
  }
  return out
}

// ───────────────────────────── ETC / EAC by function and line

export interface LineRow { line: ForecastLine; budget: Money; actual: Money; etc: Money; eac: Money; vac: Money }
export interface FunctionRow {
  fnId: string; code: string; name: string
  budget: Money; actual: Money; etc: Money; eac: Money; vac: Money // vac = budget − EAC
  pctSpent: number // actual / EAC
  profile?: FnProfile
  lines: LineRow[]
}
/** ETC and EAC by WBS function (lines nested). EAC = actuals to date + ETC. Contingency is reported separately. */
export function etcEacByFunction(s: AppState, p: Project, f?: Forecast): { rows: FunctionRow[]; contingencyHeld: Money; totals: { budget: Money; actual: Money; etc: Money; eac: Money; vac: Money } } {
  const bud = budgetByLine(s, p.id)
  const act = actualsByLine(s, p.id)
  const grid = lineGridOf(s, p, f)
  const rows: FunctionRow[] = s.settings.wbsFunctions.map((fn) => {
    const lines = linesOfFunction(s, fn.id).map((line) => {
      const budget = bud.byLine[line.id] || 0, actual = act[line.id] || 0, etc = sum(Object.values(grid[line.id] ?? {}))
      return { line, budget, actual, etc, eac: actual + etc, vac: budget - actual - etc }
    })
    const t = (k: 'budget' | 'actual' | 'etc' | 'eac' | 'vac') => sum(lines.map((l) => l[k]))
    return { fnId: fn.id, code: fn.code, name: fn.name, budget: t('budget'), actual: t('actual'), etc: t('etc'), eac: t('eac'), vac: t('vac'), pctSpent: t('eac') ? t('actual') / t('eac') : 0, profile: f?.profiles?.[fn.id], lines }
  })
  const T = (k: 'budget' | 'actual' | 'etc' | 'eac' | 'vac') => sum(rows.map((r) => r[k]))
  return { rows, contingencyHeld: f?.contingencyHeld ?? 0, totals: { budget: T('budget') + bud.contingency, actual: T('actual'), etc: T('etc'), eac: T('eac') + (f?.contingencyHeld ?? 0), vac: T('budget') + bud.contingency - T('eac') - (f?.contingencyHeld ?? 0) } }
}

/** Keep the function-level `spread`, `eac` and `byYear` in step after editing `lineSpread`. Mutates the forecast. */
export function syncForecast(s: AppState, f: Forecast) {
  if (!f.lineSpread) return
  f.spread = rollupGrid(s, f.lineSpread)
  const act = sum(s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === f.projectId).map((c) => c.actual))
  const etc = sum(Object.values(f.lineSpread).flatMap((m) => Object.values(m)))
  f.eac = act + etc + (f.contingencyHeld ?? 0)
  const byYear: Record<string, Money> = {}
  for (const c of s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === f.projectId)) byYear[c.period.slice(0, 4)] = (byYear[c.period.slice(0, 4)] || 0) + c.actual
  for (const m of Object.values(f.lineSpread)) for (const [k, v] of Object.entries(m)) byYear[k.slice(0, 4)] = (byYear[k.slice(0, 4)] || 0) + v
  // contingency held sits in the final year so the calendar years add up to EAC (same convention as the seed and applySpread)
  const ys = Object.keys(byYear).sort()
  if (f.contingencyHeld) byYear[ys[ys.length - 1] ?? s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4)] = (byYear[ys[ys.length - 1] ?? s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4)] || 0) + f.contingencyHeld
  f.byYear = byYear
}

export type { SpreadProfile }

===== FILE: src/core/engine/notify.ts =====
// Workflow reminders and escalation. There is no server, so this runs in the client: once when the app loads and
// from "Send reminders now". It is idempotent — every notice sent is recorded on the instance (data.__notices,
// keyed by the active step and the moment it became active), so a step is reminded and escalated at most once.
//
//  • Reminder   — `settings.workflow.reminderDays` days before the step's due date (0 = on the due date).
//  • Overdue    — once, the first run after the due date passes.
//  • Escalation — to the assignee's manager once the step is `settings.workflow.escalateAfterDays` days overdue
//                 (0 turns escalation off). Recorded in the workflow history as an 'escalate' event.
// Assignees who are out of office have the notice copied to their delegate.
import type { AppState, ID, ISODate, WorkflowInstance } from '../types'
import { daysBetween, fmtDate, nowISO, uid } from '../util'
import { activeDelegate } from './workflow'

type Slot = WorkflowInstance['active'][number]

export interface SlotNotices {
  due: Record<ID, ISODate> // assignee → date the due-soon reminder went out
  overdue: Record<ID, ISODate> // assignee → date the overdue reminder went out
  escalated: Record<ID, { at: ISODate; to: ID }> // assignee → when and to whom the step was escalated
}

export type NoticeKind = 'reminder' | 'overdue' | 'escalation'

export interface PlannedNotice {
  kind: NoticeKind
  instId: ID
  nodeId: ID
  slotKey: string
  assigneeId: ID // whose step it is (first, for escalations covering several assignees)
  assigneeIds: ID[] // every assignee this notice covers
  toIds: ID[] // who is notified
  stepLabel: string
  title: string
  due: ISODate
  daysLate: number // negative = days remaining
  text: string
  projectId?: ID
}

const KEY = '__notices'
export const slotKey = (a: Pick<Slot, 'nodeId' | 'since'>) => `${a.nodeId}@${a.since}`

function allNotices(inst: WorkflowInstance): Record<string, SlotNotices> {
  const v = inst.data[KEY]
  return v && typeof v === 'object' ? (v as Record<string, SlotNotices>) : {}
}

/** What has already been sent for one active step. */
export function noticesFor(inst: WorkflowInstance, slot: Pick<Slot, 'nodeId' | 'since'>): SlotNotices {
  return allNotices(inst)[slotKey(slot)] ?? { due: {}, overdue: {}, escalated: {} }
}

export const workflowNotifySettings = (s: Pick<AppState, 'settings'>) => ({
  reminderDays: Math.max(0, s.settings.workflow?.reminderDays ?? 1),
  escalateAfterDays: Math.max(0, s.settings.workflow?.escalateAfterDays ?? 3),
})

const nameOf = (s: Pick<AppState, 'people'>, id: ID) => s.people.find((p) => p.id === id)?.name ?? id
const plural = (n: number, w: string) => `${n} ${w}${n === 1 ? '' : 's'}`

/** Dry run: every notice the next run would send as of `asOf`. Pure — reads the state, changes nothing. */
export function planReminders(s: AppState, asOf: ISODate = s.settings.asOf): PlannedNotice[] {
  const { reminderDays, escalateAfterDays } = workflowNotifySettings(s)
  const view = { people: s.people, settings: { ...s.settings, asOf } }
  const out: PlannedNotice[] = []
  for (const inst of s.workflows) {
    if (inst.status !== 'running') continue
    const def = s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === inst.defId)
    for (const slot of inst.active) {
      if (!slot.due) continue
      const sent = noticesFor(inst, slot)
      const left = daysBetween(asOf, slot.due)
      const stepLabel = def?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === slot.nodeId)?.label ?? 'Step'
      const base = { instId: inst.id, nodeId: slot.nodeId, slotKey: slotKey(slot), stepLabel, title: inst.title, due: slot.due, daysLate: -left, projectId: inst.projectId }
      const escalate = new Map<ID, ID[]>() // manager → late assignees
      for (const pid of slot.assigneeIds) {
        if (slot.actedIds.includes(pid)) continue
        const del = activeDelegate(view, pid)
        const toIds = del ? [pid, del] : [pid]
        if (left >= 0 && left <= reminderDays && !sent.due[pid]) {
          const when = left === 0 ? 'today' : left === 1 ? 'tomorrow' : `in ${plural(left, 'day')}`
          out.push({ ...base, kind: 'reminder', assigneeId: pid, assigneeIds: [pid], toIds, text: `Reminder: ${stepLabel} — ${inst.title} is due ${when} (${fmtDate(slot.due)})${del ? ` · ${nameOf(s, del)} is covering for ${nameOf(s, pid)}` : ''}` })
        }
        if (left < 0 && !sent.overdue[pid]) {
          out.push({ ...base, kind: 'overdue', assigneeId: pid, assigneeIds: [pid], toIds, text: `Overdue: ${stepLabel} — ${inst.title} was due ${fmtDate(slot.due)} (${plural(-left, 'day')} late)${del ? ` · ${nameOf(s, del)} is covering for ${nameOf(s, pid)}` : ''}` })
        }
        if (escalateAfterDays > 0 && -left >= escalateAfterDays && !sent.escalated[pid]) {
          // one escalation per manager per step; a manager who is also on the step already has it in their queue
          const mgr = s.people.find((p) => p.id === pid)?.managerId
          if (!mgr || slot.assigneeIds.includes(mgr)) continue
          escalate.set(mgr, [...(escalate.get(mgr) ?? []), pid])
        }
      }
      for (const [mgr, pids] of escalate) {
        out.push({ ...base, kind: 'escalation', assigneeId: pids[0], assigneeIds: pids, toIds: [mgr], text: `Escalated: ${stepLabel} — ${inst.title} is ${plural(-left, 'day')} overdue with ${pids.map((x) => nameOf(s, x)).join(', ')}` })
      }
    }
  }
  return out
}

export interface RunResult { reminders: number; overdue: number; escalations: number; notices: PlannedNotice[] }

/** Send every due notice (mutates the immer draft) and record it so it is never sent twice. */
export function runReminders(d: AppState, asOf: ISODate = d.settings.asOf): RunResult {
  const plan = planReminders(d, asOf)
  for (const n of plan) {
    const inst = d.workflows.find((w) => w.id === n.instId)
    if (!inst) continue
    const all = allNotices(inst)
    const rec: SlotNotices = all[n.slotKey] ?? { due: {}, overdue: {}, escalated: {} }
    if (n.kind === 'reminder') rec.due[n.assigneeId] = asOf
    if (n.kind === 'overdue') { rec.overdue[n.assigneeId] = asOf; if (!rec.due[n.assigneeId]) rec.due[n.assigneeId] = asOf }
    if (n.kind === 'escalation') {
      for (const a of n.assigneeIds) rec.escalated[a] = { at: asOf, to: n.toIds[0] }
      inst.history.push({ id: uid('ev'), at: nowISO(), nodeId: n.nodeId, actorId: 'system', action: 'escalate', comment: `Escalated to ${nameOf(d, n.toIds[0])} — ${plural(n.daysLate, 'day')} overdue with ${n.assigneeIds.map((x) => nameOf(d, x)).join(', ')}` })
    }
    all[n.slotKey] = rec
    inst.data[KEY] = all
    for (const to of n.toIds) d.notifications.push({ id: uid('ntf'), at: nowISO(), toId: to, text: n.text, link: '#inbox', read: false, kind: n.kind === 'escalation' ? 'escalation' : 'reminder' })
  }
  const result = { reminders: plan.filter((n) => n.kind === 'reminder').length, overdue: plan.filter((n) => n.kind === 'overdue').length, escalations: plan.filter((n) => n.kind === 'escalation').length, notices: plan }
  if (plan.length) d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: nowISO(), actorId: 'system', entity: 'workflow', entityId: 'reminders', action: 'remind', summary: `Workflow reminders as of ${asOf}: ${result.reminders} due-soon, ${result.overdue} overdue, ${result.escalations} escalated` })
  return result
}

/** Reminder / escalation state of one person's step, for My work. */
export function taskNoticeState(inst: WorkflowInstance, slot: Pick<Slot, 'nodeId' | 'since'>, personId: ID) {
  const n = noticesFor(inst, slot)
  const escalations = Object.entries(n.escalated).map(([assigneeId, e]) => ({ assigneeId, ...e }))
  return { reminded: n.due[personId], overdueReminded: n.overdue[personId], escalated: n.escalated[personId], escalations }
}

/** Every escalation still open (the step has not been acted on), newest first — for the manager's view. */
export function openEscalations(s: AppState, managerId?: ID) {
  const out: { inst: WorkflowInstance; slot: Slot; assigneeId: ID; at: ISODate; to: ID; stepLabel: string }[] = []
  for (const inst of s.workflows) {
    if (inst.status !== 'running') continue
    const def = s.workflowDefs.find((d) => d.id === inst.defId)
    for (const slot of inst.active) {
      const seen = new Set<ID>()
      for (const [assigneeId, e] of Object.entries(noticesFor(inst, slot).escalated)) {
        if (slot.actedIds.includes(assigneeId) || seen.has(e.to) || (managerId && e.to !== managerId)) continue
        seen.add(e.to)
        out.push({ inst, slot, assigneeId, at: e.at, to: e.to, stepLabel: def?.nodes.find((n) => n.id === slot.nodeId)?.label ?? 'Step' })
      }
    }
  }
  return out.sort((a, b) => b.at.localeCompare(a.at))
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/parametric.ts =====
// Class 5 parametric request: what a PM enters (length, location, a Low/Medium/High rating per risk item, with the
// pipe and work method from the project record) priced automatically from the rate table, plus a seeded Monte Carlo.
//
//   rate-table direct  = Σ rate rows (region row, else base row × region factor) × quantity      (parametricLines)
//   risk allowances    = rate-table direct × most-likely % of each rated risk item                 (one line per item)
//   base               = direct + sales tax + loaders + escalation (rate basis year → spend midpoint)
//   Monte Carlo        = rate-table cost ~ triangular(P10, P50, P90 of the rows); each risk item ~ triangular(min, mode, max) %
//   contingency        = P80 − base (risk-based), so the estimate of record is the P80; range shown is P10–P90
//
// Risk items are physical, legal or logistical conditions only (AGENT_RULES 13–14): never demographic or wealth inputs.
import type { AppState, Estimate, EstimateLine, ParametricInputs, ParametricMonteCarlo, ParametricRecord, Project, RiskItemDef, RiskLevel } from '../types'
import { nowISO, rng, sum, triangular, uid } from '../util'
import { estimateTotals, parametricLines, regionalUnitCost } from './estimates'
import { basisYearOf, currentLoaders, escalationPctFor, salesTaxFor, spendMidpoint } from './estimatingAdmin'
import { siteOf, taxForDistrict } from './factorModel'

export const RISK_LEVELS: RiskLevel[] = ['low', 'medium', 'high']
export const RISK_LEVEL_LABEL: Record<RiskLevel, string> = { low: 'Low risk', medium: 'Medium risk', high: 'High risk' }

const lvl = (label: string, min: number, mode: number, max: number) => ({ label, min, mode, max })
/**
 * Default risk items for gas pipeline work. Impacts are triangular % of rate-table direct cost (min, most likely, max).
 * VERIFY: every item, level description and % impact is an illustrative planning value for the fictional utility; the
 * estimating team sets the real table in Estimating → Administration.
 */
export const DEFAULT_RISK_ITEMS: RiskItemDef[] = [
  { id: 'traffic', name: 'Traffic control & lane closures', help: 'How much traffic control the street and agency will require.', levels: { low: lvl('Residential or rural street, flagging only', 0, 0, 2), medium: lvl('Arterial lane closures with a traffic control plan', 2, 5, 9), high: lvl('Major arterial or near a freeway: detours, night closures', 6, 12, 22) } },
  { id: 'permits', name: 'Permitting & agency coordination', help: 'Agencies whose approval the work needs.', levels: { low: lvl('Single city or county encroachment permit', 0, 0, 2), medium: lvl('Several agencies, state highway or railroad', 2, 4, 8), high: lvl('Environmental document, coastal or federal waters permit', 5, 10, 20) } },
  { id: 'soil', name: 'Soil & rock conditions', help: 'What the trench or bore will hit.', levels: { low: lvl('Normal soil', 0, 0, 2), medium: lvl('Cobble or caliche', 2, 5, 9), high: lvl('Rock or hard dig: rock wheel or blasting', 6, 12, 25) } },
  { id: 'crossings', name: 'Road, rail & water crossings', help: 'Bores and drills along the route.', levels: { low: lvl('None, or a single bore', 0, 0, 2), medium: lvl('Two to five bores', 2, 5, 10), high: lvl('Six or more, or a drill under rail or water', 6, 12, 25) } },
  { id: 'utilities', name: 'Existing utility conflicts', help: 'Other underground facilities in the corridor.', levels: { low: lvl('Open corridor', 0, 0, 2), medium: lvl('Some potholing, crossing other utilities', 1, 4, 8), high: lvl('Congested corridor, relocations needed', 5, 10, 20) } },
  { id: 'groundwater', name: 'Groundwater & dewatering', help: 'Water expected in the excavation.', levels: { low: lvl('None expected', 0, 0, 1), medium: lvl('Seasonal or partial dewatering', 1, 3, 6), high: lvl('Continuous dewatering, treat or haul the water', 4, 8, 15) } },
  { id: 'environmental', name: 'Environmental & cultural resources', help: 'Monitoring or protection the site needs.', levels: { low: lvl('None known', 0, 0, 1), medium: lvl('Biological or cultural monitoring', 1, 3, 6), high: lvl('Sensitive habitat, contaminated soil or tribal monitoring', 4, 8, 18) } },
  { id: 'workhours', name: 'Work-hour restrictions', help: 'When crews are allowed to work.', levels: { low: lvl('Standard day shift', 0, 0, 1), medium: lvl('Restricted hours', 2, 4, 7), high: lvl('Night work only', 5, 10, 18) } },
  { id: 'tieins', name: 'Tie-ins & service transfers', help: 'Connections to the live system and customer services.', levels: { low: lvl('Few tie-ins, no customer outages', 0, 0, 2), medium: lvl('Several tie-ins, some service transfers', 1, 3, 6), high: lvl('Complex tie-ins, hot taps, many service transfers', 4, 8, 15) } },
  { id: 'access', name: 'Site access & right-of-way', help: 'Getting crews, equipment and rights to the work.', levels: { low: lvl('Public right-of-way, easy access', 0, 0, 1), medium: lvl('Temporary construction easements', 1, 3, 6), high: lvl('New permanent easements or remote access', 4, 8, 16) } },
  { id: 'restoration', name: 'Paving & restoration', help: 'What the street or site must be restored to.', levels: { low: lvl('Dirt or landscape', 0, 0, 1), medium: lvl('Standard trench paving', 1, 3, 6), high: lvl('Full-lane grind and overlay, concrete, or moratorium street', 3, 7, 14) } },
]

/** Estimate category each risk allowance is booked to. */
const RISK_CATEGORY: Record<string, string> = { traffic: 'cat-traffic', permits: 'cat-permit-fees', environmental: 'cat-env', access: 'cat-land', restoration: 'cat-restore' }
const riskCategory = (id: string) => RISK_CATEGORY[id] ?? 'cat-contract'

export const riskItemsOf = (s: Pick<AppState, 'settings'>): RiskItemDef[] => (s.settings.estimating.riskItems?.length ? s.settings.estimating.riskItems : DEFAULT_RISK_ITEMS)

/** Validation for the administration table. */
export function validateRiskItems(items: RiskItemDef[]): string[] {
  const out: string[] = []
  const ids = new Set<string>()
  for (const it of items) {
    if (!it.id || ids.has(it.id)) out.push(`Duplicate or missing id: "${it.id}"`)
    ids.add(it.id)
    if (!it.name.trim()) out.push(`${it.id}: name is required`)
    for (const k of RISK_LEVELS) {
      const l = it.levels[k]
      if (!(l.min <= l.mode && l.mode <= l.max)) out.push(`${it.name}, ${RISK_LEVEL_LABEL[k]}: needs min ≤ most likely ≤ max`)
      if (l.min < -50 || l.max > 200) out.push(`${it.name}, ${RISK_LEVEL_LABEL[k]}: impact outside −50% to +200%`)
    }
    if (/\bdac\b|disadvantag|income|wealth|race|ethnic|demograph|home.?value|zip/i.test(`${it.id} ${it.name} ${it.help}`)) out.push(`${it.name}: risk items must be physical, legal or logistical conditions`)
  }
  return out
}

// ───────────────────────────── Inputs from a project
const fromDriver = <T extends string>(v: T | undefined, map: Partial<Record<T, RiskLevel>>): RiskLevel => (v && map[v]) || 'low'

/** Risk levels pre-filled from the project's cost drivers (physical, legal and logistical fields only). */
export function risksFromProject(s: Pick<AppState, 'settings'>, p?: Project): Record<string, RiskLevel> {
  const out: Record<string, RiskLevel> = Object.fromEntries(riskItemsOf(s).map((r) => [r.id, 'low' as RiskLevel]))
  if (!p) return out
  const d = siteOf(p)
  const set = (id: string, v: RiskLevel) => { if (id in out) out[id] = v }
  set('traffic', fromDriver(d.traffic, { moderate: 'medium', heavy: 'high' }))
  set('permits', fromDriver(d.permitComplexity, { discretionary: 'medium', major: 'high' }))
  set('soil', fromDriver(d.soil, { cobble: 'medium', rock: 'high' }))
  set('crossings', d.crossings >= 6 ? 'high' : d.crossings >= 2 ? 'medium' : 'low')
  set('workhours', fromDriver(d.workHours, { restricted: 'medium', night: 'high' }))
  set('access', fromDriver(d.remoteness, { regional: 'medium', remote: 'high' }))
  set('environmental', d.hca ? 'medium' : 'low')
  set('utilities', p.location.terrain === 'urban' ? 'medium' : 'low')
  set('restoration', p.location.terrain === 'urban' ? 'high' : p.location.terrain === 'suburban' ? 'medium' : 'low')
  return out
}

/** A PM's starting inputs for a project (or defaults for a new need). */
export function parametricInputsFromProject(s: AppState, p?: Project): ParametricInputs {
  const y = Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))
  return {
    assetType: p?.asset.type ?? 'distribution-main',
    workMethod: p?.workMethod ?? 'replace-open-trench',
    diameterIn: p?.asset.diameterIn ?? 8,
    lengthFt: p?.asset.lengthFt ?? 0,
    region: p?.location.district ?? s.settings.estimating.regions[0] ?? 'East',
    midpointYear: p ? spendMidpoint(s, p) : y + 2,
    risks: risksFromProject(s, p),
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Pricing
export interface ParametricOpts { adjustPct?: number; iterations?: number; seed?: number }
export interface ParametricRiskLine { id: string; name: string; level: RiskLevel; levelLabel: string; min: number; mode: number; max: number; amount: number }
export interface ParametricResult {
  inputs: ParametricInputs
  rateLines: EstimateLine[]
  riskLines: EstimateLine[]
  adjustmentLine?: EstimateLine
  lines: EstimateLine[]
  rateDirect: number
  rateSpread: { p10: number; p90: number }
  risks: ParametricRiskLine[]
  riskPct: number
  adjustPct: number
  direct: number // rate-table direct + risk allowances + adjustment
  salesTax: NonNullable<Estimate['salesTax']>
  tax: number
  loaders: { name: string; pct: number }[]
  loaderAmount: number
  escalationPct: number
  escalation: number
  basisYear: number
  base: number // before contingency
  mc: ParametricMonteCarlo
  contingency: number
  contingencyPct: number
  total: number // = base + contingency (≈ P80)
  low: number // P10
  high: number // P90
  perFoot: number
}

/** A project-shaped object the rate-table lookup can price (pipe, method, region, setting). */
function pricingProject(i: ParametricInputs, p?: Project): Project {
  return { asset: { type: i.assetType, diameterIn: i.diameterIn, lengthFt: i.lengthFt }, workMethod: i.workMethod, location: { district: i.region, terrain: p?.location.terrain ?? 'suburban' }, scope: { quantities: [] } } as unknown as Project
}

/** Price a parametric request. Pure. Undefined when the rate table has no rows for this asset and work method. */
export function priceParametric(s: AppState, i: ParametricInputs, p?: Project, o: ParametricOpts = {}): ParametricResult | undefined {
  const opts = { region: i.region, regionFactors: s.settings.estimating.regionFactors }
  const rateLines = parametricLines(pricingProject(i, p), s.rates, opts, { lengthFt: i.lengthFt, sites: 1 }).map((l) => ({ ...l, workPackage: 'Rate table' }))
  if (!rateLines.length || !i.lengthFt) return
  const lineAt = (l: EstimateLine, k: 'p10' | 'p90') => {
    const r = s.rates.find((x) => x.id === l.rateId)
    if (!r) return l.qty * l.unitCost
    return l.qty * r[k] * regionalUnitCost(r, opts).factor
  }
  const rateDirect = Math.round(sum(rateLines.map((l) => l.qty * l.unitCost)))
  const rateSpread = { p10: Math.round(sum(rateLines.map((l) => lineAt(l, 'p10')))), p90: Math.round(sum(rateLines.map((l) => lineAt(l, 'p90')))) }

  const risks: ParametricRiskLine[] = riskItemsOf(s).map((it) => {
    const level = i.risks[it.id] ?? 'low'
    const x = it.levels[level]
    return { id: it.id, name: it.name, level, levelLabel: x.label, min: x.min, mode: x.mode, max: x.max, amount: Math.round(rateDirect * x.mode / 100) }
  })
  const riskLines: EstimateLine[] = risks.filter((r) => r.amount).map((r) => ({
    id: uid('ln'), categoryId: riskCategory(r.id), workPackage: 'Risk allowances', description: `${r.name}: ${RISK_LEVEL_LABEL[r.level].toLowerCase()} allowance (${r.mode}% of rate-table direct)`,
    qty: 1, unit: 'LS', unitCost: r.amount, source: 'judgment' as const, confidence: 'low' as const,
  }))
  const riskPct = sum(risks.map((r) => r.mode))
  const adjustPct = o.adjustPct ?? 0
  const preAdj = rateDirect + sum(riskLines.map((l) => l.unitCost))
  const adjustmentLine: EstimateLine | undefined = adjustPct ? { id: uid('ln'), categoryId: 'cat-contract', workPackage: 'Estimator adjustment', description: `Estimator adjustment ${adjustPct > 0 ? '+' : ''}${adjustPct}% of direct cost`, qty: 1, unit: 'LS', unitCost: Math.round(preAdj * adjustPct / 100), source: 'judgment', confidence: 'low' } : undefined
  const lines = [...rateLines, ...riskLines, ...(adjustmentLine ? [adjustmentLine] : [])]

  const salesTax = p ? salesTaxFor(s, p) : { pct: i.taxPct ?? taxForDistrict(s, i.region), counties: [], categoryIds: [...s.settings.estimating.taxableCategoryIds] }
  const loaders = currentLoaders(s)
  const basisYear = basisYearOf(s, rateLines)
  const escalationPct = escalationPctFor(s, basisYear, i.midpointYear)
  const t = estimateTotals({ lines, loaders, escalationPct, contingency: { method: 'deterministic', pct: 0 }, cls: 5, salesTax })
  const direct = t.lines

  // Everything after direct cost (tax on taxable rows, loaders, escalation) scales with direct cost.
  const markup = direct ? t.base / direct : 1
  const adjF = 1 + adjustPct / 100
  const iterations = o.iterations ?? s.settings.factorModel?.iterations ?? 5000
  const mc = monteCarloParametric({ rateP10: rateSpread.p10, rateP50: rateDirect, rateP90: rateSpread.p90, risks, markup: markup * adjF, rateDirect }, iterations, o.seed ?? 17)
  // VERIFY: risk-based Class 5 contingency = P80 of the Monte Carlo less the deterministic base (never negative), so the
  // estimate of record sits at P80; the range shown is the Monte Carlo P10–P90.
  const contingency = Math.max(0, mc.p.P80 - t.base)
  const contingencyPct = t.base ? Math.round((contingency / t.base) * 1000) / 10 : 0
  const total = estimateTotals({ lines, loaders, escalationPct, contingency: { method: 'risk-based', pct: contingencyPct }, cls: 5, salesTax }).total
  return {
    inputs: i, rateLines, riskLines, adjustmentLine, lines, rateDirect, rateSpread, risks, riskPct, adjustPct, direct,
    salesTax, tax: t.tax, loaders, loaderAmount: t.loaders, escalationPct, escalation: t.escalation, basisYear, base: t.base,
    mc, contingency: total - t.base, contingencyPct, total, low: Math.min(mc.p.P10, total), high: Math.max(mc.p.P90, total), perFoot: i.lengthFt ? total / i.lengthFt : 0,
  }
}

interface McInput { rateP10: number; rateP50: number; rateP90: number; risks: Pick<ParametricRiskLine, 'id' | 'name' | 'min' | 'mode' | 'max' | 'level'>[]; markup: number; rateDirect: number }

/**
 * Seeded Monte Carlo of the parametric estimate. Rate-table cost ~ triangular(P10, P50, P90 of the rows); each risk item
 * at its rated level ~ triangular(min, most likely, max) % of rate-table direct, independent; tax, loaders, escalation
 * and any estimator adjustment scale each draw. Pure: the same inputs and seed give the same answer.
 * VERIFY: independence between risk items and the triangular shapes are modelling assumptions for the estimating team.
 */
export function monteCarloParametric(x: McInput, iterations = 5000, seed = 17): ParametricMonteCarlo {
  const R = rng(seed)
  const lo10 = Math.min(x.rateP10, x.rateP50), hi90 = Math.max(x.rateP90, x.rateP50)
  const active = x.risks.filter((r) => r.max > r.min || r.mode)
  const xs: number[] = new Array(iterations)
  for (let k = 0; k < iterations; k++) {
    const rate = triangular(R.next(), lo10, x.rateP50, hi90)
    let pct = 0
    for (const r of active) pct += triangular(R.next(), r.min, r.mode, r.max)
    xs[k] = (rate + x.rateDirect * pct / 100) * x.markup
  }
  xs.sort((a, b) => a - b)
  const q = (p: number) => Math.round(xs[Math.min(xs.length - 1, Math.max(0, Math.floor(p * xs.length)))])
  const lo = xs[0], hi = xs[xs.length - 1]
  const bins = 30
  const w = (hi - lo) / bins || 1
  const histogram = Array.from({ length: bins }, (_, k) => ({ x: Math.round(lo + w * (k + 0.5)), n: 0 }))
  for (const v of xs) histogram[Math.min(bins - 1, Math.floor((v - lo) / w))].n++
  const scurve = Array.from({ length: 21 }, (_, k) => ({ pct: k * 5, value: q(Math.min(0.999, Math.max(0.001, k / 20))) }))

  // Tornado: each source swung from its low to its high end with everything else at most likely.
  const modeSum = sum(active.map((r) => r.mode))
  const at = (rate: number, pct: number) => Math.round((rate + x.rateDirect * pct / 100) * x.markup)
  const rows = [
    { id: 'rate-table', label: 'Rate-table unit costs (P10–P90 of the rows)', low: at(lo10, modeSum), high: at(hi90, modeSum) },
    ...active.map((r) => ({ id: r.id, label: `${r.name} (${RISK_LEVEL_LABEL[r.level].toLowerCase()})`, low: at(x.rateP50, modeSum - r.mode + r.min), high: at(x.rateP50, modeSum - r.mode + r.max) })),
  ].map((t) => ({ ...t, swing: t.high - t.low }))
  const tot = sum(rows.map((t) => t.swing ** 2)) || 1
  const tornado = rows.map((t) => ({ ...t, share: t.swing ** 2 / tot })).filter((t) => t.swing > 0).sort((a, b) => b.swing - a.swing)
  return { iterations, seed, p: { P10: q(0.1), P50: q(0.5), P80: q(0.8), P90: q(0.9) }, mean: Math.round(sum(xs) / (xs.length || 1)), histogram, scurve, tornado }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Basis of estimate
const money = (n: number) => '$' + (Math.abs(n) >= 1e6 ? (n / 1e6).toFixed(2) + 'M' : Math.round(n).toLocaleString('en-US'))

/** Generated basis of estimate. VERIFY: BOE wording for parametric Class 5 requests (scope, assumptions, contingency, class). */
export function parametricBoe(r: ParametricResult, adjustment?: { pct: number; reason: string; changed?: string[] }): Estimate['basis'] {
  const i = r.inputs
  const rated = r.risks.filter((x) => x.level !== 'low')
  const assumptions = [
    `Rate table: ${r.rateLines.length} rate rows for ${i.diameterIn}" ${i.assetType.replace(/-/g, ' ')}, ${i.workMethod.replace(/-/g, ' ')}, ${i.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft in the ${i.region} region (region rows, else base rate × region factor): ${money(r.rateDirect)} direct at P50, ${money(r.rateSpread.p10)}–${money(r.rateSpread.p90)} at the rows' P10–P90.`,
    rated.length ? `Risk allowances (most likely % of rate-table direct): ${rated.map((x) => `${x.name} ${x.level} (${x.mode}%, ${x.min}–${x.max}%)`).join('; ')}. All other items rated low.` : 'Every risk item rated low: no allowances above the rate table.',
    `Sales tax ${r.salesTax.pct}% on taxable material; loaders ${sum(r.loaders.map((l) => l.pct)).toFixed(1)}%; escalation ${r.escalationPct}% from the ${r.basisYear} rate basis to the ${i.midpointYear} spend midpoint.`,
  ]
  if (adjustment && (adjustment.pct || adjustment.changed?.length)) assumptions.push(`Estimator review: ${[adjustment.changed?.length ? `risk ratings changed (${adjustment.changed.join('; ')})` : '', adjustment.pct ? `${adjustment.pct > 0 ? '+' : ''}${adjustment.pct}% adjustment` : ''].filter(Boolean).join(', ')} — ${adjustment.reason}.`)
  return {
    scopeBasis: `${i.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft of ${i.diameterIn}" ${i.assetType.replace(/-/g, ' ')}, ${i.workMethod.replace(/-/g, ' ')}, ${i.region} region. Class 5 parametric estimate from the rate table and the PM's risk ratings.`,
    assumptions,
    exclusions: ['Scope not described by the rate rows or the rated risk items', 'Land acquisition by condemnation', 'Unknown contamination remediation'],
    contingencyRationale: `Risk-based: Monte Carlo of ${r.mc.iterations.toLocaleString('en-US')} seeded draws (rate-table cost triangular on the rows' P10/P50/P90; each risk item triangular on its min/most likely/max, independent). Funded at P80 ${money(r.mc.p.P80)}: contingency ${money(r.contingency)} (${r.contingencyPct}% of base). P10–P90 ${money(r.mc.p.P10)}–${money(r.mc.p.P90)}.`,
    classJustification: 'Class 5: need, location and approximate length known; no design. Parametric from the rate table with rated physical, legal and logistical risks.',
  }
}

// ───────────────────────────── Saved estimates
function record(s: AppState, r: ParametricResult, prev?: ParametricRecord['adjustment'], requested?: Record<string, RiskLevel>): ParametricRecord {
  return {
    inputs: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(r.inputs)), requestedRisks: { ...(requested ?? r.inputs.risks) }, rateTableVersion: s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion, rateDirect: r.rateDirect, rateSpread: r.rateSpread,
    risks: r.risks.map((x) => ({ id: x.id, name: x.name, level: x.level, pct: x.mode, amount: x.amount })), base: r.base,
    mc: { iterations: r.mc.iterations, seed: r.mc.seed, p: r.mc.p, mean: r.mc.mean }, contingencyPct: r.contingencyPct, adjustment: prev,
  }
}

function applyResult(s: AppState, e: Estimate, r: ParametricResult) {
  e.lines = r.lines
  e.loaders = r.loaders
  e.escalationPct = r.escalationPct
  e.contingency = { method: 'risk-based', pct: r.contingencyPct, confidence: 'P80' }
  e.salesTax = r.salesTax
  e.region = r.inputs.region
  e.rateTableVersion = s.settings.estimating.rateTableVersion
}

/** Create a draft Class 5 parametric estimate on a project (route it with class5-review). Mutates the draft. */
export function createParametricEstimate(d: AppState, pid: string, i: ParametricInputs, requestNote: string, byId = d.currentUserId, needBy?: string): Estimate | undefined {
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)
  if (!p) return
  const r = priceParametric(d, i, p)
  if (!r) return
  const version = Math.max(0, ...d.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid).map((e) => e.version)) + 1
  const e: Estimate = {
    id: uid('est'), projectId: pid, version, title: 'Class 5 parametric estimate', cls: 5, status: 'draft', method: 'quick',
    createdAt: nowISO(), createdById: byId, lines: [], maturity: { 'need-defined': true }, loaders: [], escalationPct: 0, contingency: { method: 'risk-based', pct: 0 },
    basis: parametricBoe(r),
    quick: { inputs: { assetType: i.assetType, workMethod: i.workMethod, diameterBand: 'n/a', terrain: p.location.terrain, district: i.region, qty: i.lengthFt, unit: 'ft', midpointYear: i.midpointYear }, historicalCostId: '', basis: 'p50', baseRate: i.lengthFt ? r.rateDirect / i.lengthFt : 0, regionFactor: d.settings.estimating.regionFactors[i.region] ?? 1, escalationFactor: 1 + r.escalationPct / 100, direct: r.direct, range: { low: r.low, high: r.high }, requestNote, needBy },
    parametric: record(d, r),
  }
  applyResult(d, e, r)
  d.estimates.push(e)
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: e.createdAt, actorId: byId, entity: 'estimate', entityId: e.id, projectId: pid, action: 'create', summary: `Class 5 parametric estimate v${version}: ${i.lengthFt.toLocaleString('en-US')} ft, ${i.region}, ${r.risks.filter((x) => x.level !== 'low').length} risk items above low — $${(r.total / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M at P80 (P10–P90 $${(r.low / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M–$${(r.high / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M)` })
  return e
}

/** Reprice a saved parametric estimate with new risk ratings and/or an estimator % (preview; no change). */
export function previewParametric(s: AppState, e: Estimate, risks: Record<string, RiskLevel>, adjustPct: number): ParametricResult | undefined {
  if (!e.parametric) return
  const p = s.projects.find((x) => x.id === e.projectId)
  return priceParametric(s, { ...e.parametric.inputs, risks: { ...e.parametric.inputs.risks, ...risks } }, p, { adjustPct })
}

/**
 * Estimating-team review of a parametric estimate: change any risk rating and/or apply a ± % with a reason. Repriced
 * (Monte Carlo included); before/after kept on the record and in the audit trail.
 */
export function adjustParametricEstimate(d: AppState, estimateId: string, o: { risks?: Record<string, RiskLevel>; pct: number; reason: string; note?: string }, byId = d.currentUserId) {
  const e = d.estimates.find((x) => x.id === estimateId)
  if (!e?.parametric || !e.quick) return
  const prevRisks = e.parametric.inputs.risks
  const risks = { ...prevRisks, ...(o.risks ?? {}) }
  const r = previewParametric(d, e, risks, o.pct)
  if (!r) return
  const names = Object.fromEntries(riskItemsOf(d).map((x) => [x.id, x.name]))
  const changed = Object.keys(risks).filter((k) => risks[k] !== prevRisks[k]).map((k) => `${names[k] ?? k} ${prevRisks[k] ?? 'low'} → ${risks[k]}`)
  const before = estimateTotals(e).total
  applyResult(d, e, r)
  const after = estimateTotals(e).total
  const reason = o.note?.trim() ? `${o.reason} — ${o.note.trim()}` : o.reason
  const adjustment = { pct: o.pct, reason, byId, at: nowISO(), fromTotal: before, toTotal: after, changed }
  e.basis = parametricBoe(r, { pct: o.pct, reason, changed })
  e.parametric = record(d, r, adjustment, e.parametric.requestedRisks ?? prevRisks)
  e.quick = { ...e.quick, direct: r.direct, range: { low: r.low, high: r.high }, adjustment: { pct: o.pct, reason, byId, at: adjustment.at, fromTotal: before, toTotal: after } }
  d.audit.push({ id: uid('aud'), at: adjustment.at, actorId: byId, entity: 'estimate', entityId: e.id, projectId: e.projectId, action: 'adjust', summary: `Estimator adjusted Class 5 parametric estimate${changed.length ? ` (${changed.join('; ')})` : ''} ${o.pct >= 0 ? '+' : ''}${o.pct}%: $${(before / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M → $${(after / 1e6).toFixed(2)}M — ${reason}` })
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/position.ts =====
// Project financial position and snapshots (Module 3).
import type { AppState, BudgetVersion, Milestone, Money, Project, Snapshot, SnapshotType } from '../types'
import { daysBetween, sum, uid } from '../util'
import { exposure } from './risk'

export const projectBudgets = (s: AppState, pid: string) => s.budgets.filter((b) => b.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.createdAt.localeCompare(b.createdAt))

/** The budget in force: latest control budget, else latest planning budget. */
export function activeBudget(s: AppState, pid: string): BudgetVersion | undefined {
  const bs = projectBudgets(s, pid)
  return [...bs].reverse().find((b) => b.kind === 'control') ?? [...bs].reverse().find((b) => b.kind === 'planning')
}

export interface Position {
  budgetKind?: 'planning' | 'control'
  originalBudget: Money // first control budget total (or planning if none)
  currentBudget: Money // incl. contingency and approved new funding
  baseBudget: Money // currentBudget excluding contingency remaining
  contingencyOriginal: Money
  contingencyUsed: Money
  contingencyRemaining: Money
  approvedChanges: Money
  pendingChanges: Money
  actualToDate: Money
  commitments: Money
  eac: Money
  variance: Money // currentBudget - eac (negative = overrun)
  riskExposure: Money
}

export function position(s: AppState, pid: string): Position {
  const b = activeBudget(s, pid)
  const firstControl = projectBudgets(s, pid).find((x) => x.kind === 'control')
  const after = (d: string) => !b || d >= b.createdAt
  const changes = s.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid)
  const approved = changes.filter((c) => c.status === 'approved' && after(c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt))
  const newFunding = sum(approved.filter((c) => c.fundedFrom === 'new-funding').map((c) => c.costImpact))
  const fromCont = sum(approved.filter((c) => c.fundedFrom === 'contingency').map((c) => c.costImpact))
  const draws = sum(s.contingencyDraws.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid && x.status === 'approved' && !x.changeId && after(x.date)).map((x) => x.amount))
  const contingencyOriginal = b?.contingency ?? 0
  const contingencyUsed = fromCont + draws
  const currentBudget = (b?.total ?? 0) + newFunding
  const periods = s.costPeriods.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid)
  const actualToDate = sum(periods.map((p) => p.actual))
  const lastPeriod = periods.slice().sort((a, b) => a.period.localeCompare(b.period)).pop()
  const fc = s.forecasts.filter((f) => f.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date)).pop()
  const eac = fc?.eac ?? currentBudget - Math.max(0, contingencyOriginal - contingencyUsed)
  const risks = s.risks.filter((r) => r.projectId === pid)
  return {
    budgetKind: b?.kind,
    originalBudget: (firstControl ?? b)?.total ?? 0,
    currentBudget,
    baseBudget: currentBudget - Math.max(0, contingencyOriginal - contingencyUsed),
    contingencyOriginal,
    contingencyUsed,
    contingencyRemaining: contingencyOriginal - contingencyUsed,
    approvedChanges: sum(approved.map((c) => c.costImpact)),
    pendingChanges: sum(changes.filter((c) => c.status === 'in-review' || c.status === 'draft').map((c) => c.costImpact)),
    actualToDate,
    commitments: lastPeriod?.commitments ?? 0,
    eac,
    variance: currentBudget - eac,
    riskExposure: exposure(risks),
  }
}

/** Freeze scope, schedule and budget into an immutable snapshot (BL-*). Mutates the draft. */
export function takeSnapshot(d: AppState, pid: string, type: SnapshotType, label: string, opts: { changeId?: string; proceeding?: string; byId?: string; date?: string } = {}): Snapshot {
  const p = d.projects.find((x) => x.id === pid)!
  const pos = position(d, pid)
  const b = activeBudget(d, pid)
  const byCategory: Record<string, Money> = { ...(b?.byCategory ?? {}) }
  // fold approved changes into their categories
  for (const c of d.changes.filter((c) => c.projectId === pid && c.status === 'approved' && (!b || (c.decidedAt ?? c.requestedAt) >= b.createdAt))) {
    byCategory[c.categoryId] = (byCategory[c.categoryId] || 0) + c.costImpact
  }
  const snap: Snapshot = {
    id: uid('snap'),
    projectId: pid,
    type,
    label,
    date: opts.date ?? d.settings.asOf,
    createdById: opts.byId ?? d.currentUserId,
    proceeding: opts.proceeding,
    changeId: opts.changeId,
    stageIndex: p.stageIndex,
    scope: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.scope)),
    milestones: JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(p.milestones)),
    budget: { byCategory, contingencyRemaining: pos.contingencyRemaining, total: pos.currentBudget, eac: pos.eac, actualToDate: pos.actualToDate },
    riskExposure: pos.riskExposure,
  }
  d.snapshots.push(snap)
  return snap
}

export const snapshotsFor = (s: AppState, pid: string) => s.snapshots.filter((x) => x.projectId === pid).sort((a, b) => a.date.localeCompare(b.date) || a.type.localeCompare(b.type))
export const latestOf = (s: AppState, pid: string, type: SnapshotType) => snapshotsFor(s, pid).filter((x) => x.type === type).pop()

export interface SnapshotDiff {
  totalDelta: Money
  eacDelta: Money
  contingencyDelta: Money
  costBridge: { categoryId: string; delta: Money }[]
  milestoneMoves: { name: string; from: string; to: string; days: number }[]
  scopeAdded: string[]
  scopeRemoved: string[]
  quantityChanges: { label: string; from: number; to: number; unit: string }[]
}

/** Compare any two snapshots (BL-04). */
export function compareSnapshots(a: Snapshot, b: Snapshot): SnapshotDiff {
  const cats = Array.from(new Set([...Object.keys(a.budget.byCategory), ...Object.keys(b.budget.byCategory)]))
  const costBridge = cats.map((c) => ({ categoryId: c, delta: (b.budget.byCategory[c] || 0) - (a.budget.byCategory[c] || 0) })).filter((x) => x.delta !== 0)
  const milestoneMoves = b.milestones
    .map((m) => {
      const prev = a.milestones.find((x) => x.name === m.name)
      return prev ? { name: m.name, from: prev.forecast, to: m.forecast, days: daysBetween(prev.forecast, m.forecast) } : null
    })
    .filter((x): x is NonNullable<typeof x> => !!x && x.days !== 0)
  const qa = new Map(a.scope.quantities.map((q) => [q.label, q]))
  return {
    totalDelta: b.budget.total - a.budget.total,
    eacDelta: b.budget.eac - a.budget.eac,
    contingencyDelta: b.budget.contingencyRemaining - a.budget.contingencyRemaining,
    costBridge,
    milestoneMoves,
    scopeAdded: b.scope.inScope.filter((x) => !a.scope.inScope.includes(x)),
    scopeRemoved: a.scope.inScope.filter((x) => !b.scope.inScope.includes(x)),
    quantityChanges: b.scope.quantities.filter((q) => qa.has(q.label) && qa.get(q.label)!.qty !== q.qty).map((q) => ({ label: q.label, from: qa.get(q.label)!.qty, to: q.qty, unit: q.unit })),
  }
}

/** Threshold check (BL-05): returns breaches needing a variance explanation. */
export function thresholdBreaches(s: AppState, p: Project): { metric: 'eac' | 'milestone'; description: string; amount: number }[] {
  const out: { metric: 'eac' | 'milestone'; description: string; amount: number }[] = []
  const pos = position(s, p.id)
  const cur = latestOf(s, p.id, 'current') ?? latestOf(s, p.id, 'original')
  const t = s.settings.varianceThresholds
  if (cur) {
    const over = pos.eac - cur.budget.total
    if (over > 0 && (over / Math.max(1, cur.budget.total) > t.eacPct / 100 || over > t.eacAbs)) out.push({ metric: 'eac', description: `EAC exceeds Current baseline by ${Math.round((over / cur.budget.total) * 100)}%`, amount: over })
    for (const m of p.milestones) {
      const bm = cur.milestones.find((x) => x.name === m.name)
      if (bm && !m.actual) {
        const slip = daysBetween(bm.forecast, m.forecast)
        if (slip > t.milestoneDays) out.push({ metric: 'milestone', description: `${m.name} slipped ${slip} days vs Current baseline`, amount: slip })
      }
    }
  }
  return out
}

export function nextMilestone(p: Project): Milestone | undefined {
  return p.milestones.filter((m) => !m.actual).sort((a, b) => a.forecast.localeCompare(b.forecast))[0]
}

===== FILE: src/core/engine/quick.ts =====
// Class 5 quick estimate: a few dropdowns → a row in the historical cost table → a forward-looking estimate
// (escalated to the spend midpoint, regionally adjusted) with a P10–P90 range. Becomes an ordinary Estimate
// (method 'quick') so it can route for review, be adjusted by the estimating team and convert to a budget.
import type { AppState, DiameterBand, Estimate, EstimateLine, HistoricalCost, HistUnit, Money, Project, QuickEstimate, QuickInputs, WorkMethod, AssetType } from '../types'
import { nowISO, sum, uid } from '../util'
import { estimateTotals } from './estimates'

export const DIAMETER_BANDS: { id: DiameterBand; label: string; min: number; max: number }[] = [
  { id: 'le-8', label: '8" and smaller', min: 0, max: 8 },
  { id: '10-16', label: '10"–16"', min: 9, max: 16 },
  { id: '20-24', label: '20"–24"', min: 17, max: 24 },
  { id: '26-36', label: '26"–36"', min: 25, max: 99 },
  { id: 'n/a', label: 'Not applicable', min: -1, max: -1 },
]
export const bandOf = (dia: number): DiameterBand => DIAMETER_BANDS.find((b) => dia >= b.min && dia <= b.max && b.id !== 'n/a')?.id ?? 'n/a'

export const UNIT_LABEL: Record<HistUnit, string> = { ft: 'feet', site: 'valve sites', hp: 'horsepower', station: 'stations' }

/** Work methods offered per asset type, and the unit the historical table is kept in. */
export const QUICK_CATALOG: { asset: AssetType; label: string; methods: WorkMethod[]; unit: HistUnit; banded: boolean }[] = [
  { asset: 'transmission-pipeline', label: 'Transmission pipeline', methods: ['replace-open-trench', 'replace-hdd', 'hydrotest'], unit: 'ft', banded: true },
  { asset: 'distribution-main', label: 'Distribution main', methods: ['replace-open-trench', 'replace-hdd'], unit: 'ft', banded: true },
  { asset: 'valve-site', label: 'Valve site', methods: ['valve-automation'], unit: 'site', banded: true },
  { asset: 'compressor-station', label: 'Compressor station', methods: ['compressor-replacement', 'station-rebuild'], unit: 'hp', banded: false },
  { asset: 'regulator-station', label: 'Regulator station', methods: ['station-rebuild'], unit: 'station', banded: false },
]
export const unitFor = (asset: AssetType): HistUnit => QUICK_CATALOG.find((c) => c.asset === asset)?.unit ?? 'ft'

/**
 * Best historical row for the inputs: exact asset/method; diameter band exact (or n/a for unbanded);
 * terrain exact, else 'any'. Returns the row and how it matched so the reviewer sees the fallback.
 */
export function findHistorical(s: AppState, i: Pick<QuickInputs, 'assetType' | 'workMethod' | 'diameterBand' | 'terrain'>): { row?: HistoricalCost; match: 'exact' | 'any-terrain' | 'nearest-band' | 'none' } {
  const pool = s.historicalCosts.filter((h) => h.active && h.assetType === i.assetType && h.workMethod === i.workMethod)
  const banded = QUICK_CATALOG.find((c) => c.asset === i.assetType)?.banded
  const band = banded ? i.diameterBand : 'n/a'
  const exact = pool.find((h) => h.diameterBand === band && h.terrain === i.terrain)
  if (exact) return { row: exact, match: 'exact' }
  const any = pool.find((h) => h.diameterBand === band && h.terrain === 'any')
  if (any) return { row: any, match: 'any-terrain' }
  // A row kept without a diameter band covers every diameter (e.g. hydrotest, valve automation): not a band fallback.
  const allDia = pool.find((h) => h.diameterBand === 'n/a' && h.terrain === i.terrain) ?? pool.find((h) => h.diameterBand === 'n/a' && h.terrain === 'any')
  if (allDia) return { row: allDia, match: allDia.terrain === 'any' ? 'any-terrain' : 'exact' }
  const order = DIAMETER_BANDS.map((b) => b.id)
  const near = pool.filter((h) => h.terrain === i.terrain || h.terrain === 'any').sort((a, b) => Math.abs(order.indexOf(a.diameterBand) - order.indexOf(band)) - Math.abs(order.indexOf(b.diameterBand) - order.indexOf(band)))[0]
  return near ? { row: near, match: 'nearest-band' } : { match: 'none' }
}

/** Compound escalation from base year to the spend midpoint using the configured escalation table. */
// VERIFY: escalation compounds the configured annual index year over year from the historical cost year to the estimate year.
export function escalationFactor(s: AppState, fromYear: number, toYear: number): number {
  let f = 1
  for (let y = fromYear + 1; y <= toYear; y++) f *= 1 + (s.settings.escalationByYear[String(y)] ?? s.settings.escalationByYear[String(Math.max(...Object.keys(s.settings.escalationByYear).map(Number)))] ?? 3) / 100
  return f
}

/** Region factor from Estimating administration (falls back to the older quick-estimate copy of the table). */
export const regionFactor = (s: AppState, district: string) => s.settings.estimating?.regionFactors?.[district] ?? s.settings.quickEstimate.regionFactors[district] ?? 1

/** Class 5 contingency: the estimating team's contingency-by-class table, else the quick-estimate default. */
export const quickContingencyPct = (s: AppState) => s.settings.estimating?.contingencyByClass?.['5'] ?? s.settings.quickEstimate.contingencyPct

export const loaderPct = (s: AppState) => {
  const latest = Math.max(...s.settings.loaderRates.map((r) => r.year))
  return s.settings.loaderRates.filter((r) => r.year === latest)
}

export interface QuickResult {
  row?: HistoricalCost
  match: ReturnType<typeof findHistorical>['match']
  baseRate: number
  regionFactor: number
  escalationFactor: number
  direct: Money // base-year rate × qty × region, escalated
  loaders: Money
  contingencyPct: number
  contingency: Money
  total: Money
  low: Money // P10
  high: Money // P90
  perUnit: number // total / qty
}

/** Compute a quick estimate from inputs. Pure. */
export function computeQuick(s: AppState, i: QuickInputs, basis: QuickEstimate['basis'] = 'p50', adjustPct = 0): QuickResult {
  const { row, match } = findHistorical(s, i)
  const rf = regionFactor(s, i.district)
  const ef = row ? escalationFactor(s, row.baseYear, i.midpointYear) : 1
  const lp = sum(loaderPct(s).map((x) => x.pct)) / 100
  const cp = quickContingencyPct(s)
  const at = (rate: number) => {
    const direct = Math.round(rate * i.qty * rf * ef * (1 + adjustPct / 100))
    const loaders = Math.round(direct * lp)
    const contingency = Math.round((direct + loaders) * cp / 100)
    return { direct, loaders, contingency, total: direct + loaders + contingency }
  }
  const mid = at(row?.[basis] ?? 0)
  return { row, match, baseRate: row?.[basis] ?? 0, regionFactor: rf, escalationFactor: ef, direct: mid.direct, loaders: mid.loaders, contingencyPct: cp, contingency: mid.contingency, total: mid.total, low: at(row?.p10 ?? 0).total, high: at(row?.p90 ?? 0).total, perUnit: i.qty ? mid.total / i.qty : 0 }
}

/** Estimate lines from the historical cost mix (one lump-sum line per category). */
export function quickLines(row: HistoricalCost, direct: Money): EstimateLine[] {
  const entries = Object.entries(row.mix).filter(([, v]) => v > 0)
  const W = sum(entries.map(([, v]) => v)) || 1
  let acc = 0
  return entries.map(([cat, v], k) => {
    const amt = k === entries.length - 1 ? direct - acc : Math.round((direct * v) / W)
    acc += amt
    return { id: uid('el'), categoryId: cat, workPackage: 'Class 5 historical', description: `Historical ${Math.round((v / W) * 100)}% share`, qty: 1, unit: 'LS', unitCost: amt, source: 'analog' as const, confidence: 'low' as const }
  })
}

/** Inputs pre-filled from a project. */
export function inputsFromProject(s: AppState, p: Project): QuickInputs {
  const unit = unitFor(p.asset.type)
  const qty = unit === 'ft' ? p.asset.lengthFt : unit === 'hp' ? (p.scope.quantities.find((q) => q.unit === 'hp')?.qty ?? 10000) : unit === 'site' ? (p.scope.quantities.find((q) => /valve/i.test(q.label))?.qty ?? 1) : 1
  const end = p.milestones.find((m) => /in.service/i.test(m.name))?.forecast ?? s.settings.asOf
  const mid = Math.round((Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4)) + Number(end.slice(0, 4))) / 2)
  return { assetType: p.asset.type, workMethod: p.workMethod, diameterBand: QUICK_CATALOG.find((c) => c.asset === p.asset.type)?.banded ? bandOf(p.asset.diameterIn) : 'n/a', terrain: p.location.terrain, district: p.location.district, qty, unit, midpointYear: Math.max(mid, Number(s.settings.asOf.slice(0, 4))) }
}

/**
 * Create a draft quick estimate on a project. Mutates the draft; returns the estimate.
 * Route it with startWorkflow(d, 'class5-review', 'estimate', e.id, …) to send it to the estimating team.
 */
export function createQuickEstimate(d: AppState, pid: string, i: QuickInputs, requestNote: string, byId = d.currentUserId, needBy?: string): Estimate | undefined {
  const r = computeQuick(d, i)
  if (!r.row) return
  const version = Math.max(0, ...d.estimates.filter((e) => e.projectId === pid).map((e) => e.version)) + 1
  const e: Estimate = {
    id: uid('est'), projectId: pid, version, title: 'Class 5 quick estimate', cls: 5, status: 'draft', method: 'quick',
    createdAt: nowISO(), createdById: byId,
    lines: quickLines(r.row, r.direct),
    maturity: { 'need-defined': true },
    loaders: loaderPct(d).map((x) => ({ name: x.name, pct: x.pct })),
    escalationPct: 0, // already in the escalated historical rate
    contingency: { method: 'deterministic', pct: r.contingencyPct },
    basis: {
      scopeBasis: `${QUICK_CATALOG.find((c) => c.asset === i.assetType)?.label ?? i.assetType}, ${i.qty.toLocaleString('en-US')} ${UNIT_LABEL[i.unit]}, ${i.terrain}, ${i.district}.`,
      assumptions: [
        `Historical direct cost ${fmtRate(r.baseRate, i.unit)} (P50 of ${r.row.n} closed projects, ${r.row.baseYear} dollars)${r.match !== 'exact' ? ` — matched on ${r.match === 'any-terrain' ? 'all terrains' : 'nearest diameter band'}` : ''}.`,
        `Escalated ${r.row.baseYear} → ${i.midpointYear} spend midpoint (×${r.escalationFactor.toFixed(3)}); ${i.district} regional factor ×${r.regionFactor.toFixed(2)}.`,
        `Loaders at current rates; contingency ${r.contingencyPct}% (Class 5 default).`,
      ],
      exclusions: ['Scope not represented in the historical population', 'Land acquisition by condemnation', 'Unknown contamination remediation'],
      contingencyRationale: `Class 5 default ${r.contingencyPct}%. Range shown is P10–P90 of the historical population.`,
      classJustification: 'Class 5: need, driver and asset identified; no design. Parametric from historical cost.',
    },
    quick: { inputs: i, historicalCostId: r.row.id, basis: 'p50', baseRate: r.baseRate, regionFactor: r.regionFactor, escalationFactor: r.escalationFactor, direct: r.direct, range: { low: r.low, high: r.high }, requestNote, needBy },
  }
  d.estimates.push(e)
