export function simulate(s: AppState, def: WorkflowDef, data: Record<string, unknown>, projectId?: string, startedById?: string): { node: WfNode; people: Person[] }[] {
  const fake: WorkflowInstance = { id: 'sim', defId: def.id, defKey: def.key, defVersion: def.version, title: 'Simulation', recordType: def.recordType, recordId: 'sim', projectId, status: 'running', active: [], startedAt: nowISO(), startedById: startedById ?? s.currentUserId, data, history: [] }
  const out: { node: WfNode; people: Person[] }[] = []
  const seen = new Set<string>()
  const walk = (id: string, depth: number) => {
    if (depth > 40) return
    const n = def.nodes.find((x) => x.id === id)
    if (!n) return
    if (seen.has(id) && n.type !== 'parallel-join') return
    seen.add(id)
    if (n.type === 'approval' || n.type === 'review' || n.type === 'form' || n.type === 'notify') {
      out.push({ node: n, people: resolveAssignees(s, fake, n.assignee).map((pid) => s.people.find((p) => p.id === pid)!).filter(Boolean) })
    }
    const edges = def.edges.filter((e) => e.from === id)
    if (n.type === 'condition') {
      const ctx = { ...data, project: projectId ? s.projects.find((p) => p.id === projectId) : undefined }
      const hit = edges.find((e) => e.condition && evalCondition(e.condition, ctx)) ?? edges.find((e) => !e.condition)
      if (hit) walk(hit.to, depth + 1)
    } else if (n.type === 'parallel-split') edges.forEach((e) => walk(e.to, depth + 1))
    else {
      const e = edges.find((e) => !e.outcome || e.outcome === 'approve' || e.outcome === 'default')
      if (e) walk(e.to, depth + 1)
    }
  }
  walk(def.nodes.find((n) => n.type === 'start')!.id, 0)
  return out
}

/** Swimlane label for a node (documentation export). */
export function laneOf(s: AppState, n: WfNode): string {
  if (n.lane) return n.lane
  const a = n.assignee
  if (!a) return 'System'
  switch (a.kind) {
    case 'role': return s.roles.find((r) => r.id === a.roleId)?.name ?? a.roleId
    case 'team-position': return ({ pm: 'Project Manager', sponsor: 'Project Sponsor', projectControls: 'Project Controls', constructionManager: 'Construction Manager', safety: 'Safety Advisor', estimator: 'Estimator', engineer: 'Engineer', scheduler: 'Scheduler', environmental: 'Environmental', land: 'Land' } as Record<string, string>)[a.position] ?? a.position
    case 'doa': return 'Approver by dollar authority'
    case 'manager-of-requester': return "Requester's manager"
    case 'requester': return 'Requester'
    case 'person': return s.people.find((p) => p.id === a.personId)?.name ?? 'Person'
  }
}

===== FILE: src/core/ai.ts =====
// Optional AI assist. Off by default; an administrator points it at the organization's approved LLM
// gateway (any OpenAI-compatible /chat/completions endpoint, e.g. Azure OpenAI behind the API gateway)
// in Configuration → AI assist. When it is off, every AI control is hidden and nothing leaves the page.
// AI only drafts; people review and accept. Every call must come from a user click.
//   const text = await ask(prompt, { onText: t => setDraft(t) })
//   const data = await askJSON<{ risks: … }>(prompt)
import { getState, useStore } from './store'

export class AiError extends Error { constructor(public code: string, message: string, public partial?: string) { super(message) } }

export const AI_MESSAGES: Record<string, string> = {
  unavailable: 'AI assist is turned off. An administrator can connect it in Configuration → AI assist.',
  http: 'The AI service returned an error. Check the endpoint in Configuration → AI assist.',
  rate_limited: 'Too many AI requests at once. Wait a moment and try again.',
  cancelled: 'Stopped.',
}
export const aiMessage = (e: unknown) => (e instanceof AiError ? (e.code === 'http' ? `${AI_MESSAGES.http} (${e.message})` : AI_MESSAGES[e.code] ?? e.message) : 'Something went wrong drafting this. Try again.')

type Msg = { role: 'system' | 'user' | 'assistant' | 'tool'; content: string | null; tool_calls?: any[]; tool_call_id?: string }
export interface AiTool { name: string; description: string; inputSchema?: Record<string, unknown>; execute: (input: Record<string, unknown>) => unknown }
export interface AskOptions {
  onText?: (text: string) => void
  signal?: AbortSignal
  tier?: 'quick' | 'default' | 'complex'
  tools?: AiTool[]
  cache?: boolean
}

function config() {
  const a = getState().settings.ai
  if (!a?.enabled || !a.endpoint) throw new AiError('unavailable', AI_MESSAGES.unavailable)
  return a
}

async function complete(messages: Msg[], o: AskOptions, json = false): Promise<Msg> {
  const a = config()
  const body: Record<string, unknown> = { model: a.model || undefined, messages, temperature: 0.2 }
  if (o.tools?.length) body.tools = o.tools.map((t) => ({ type: 'function', function: { name: t.name, description: t.description, parameters: t.inputSchema ?? { type: 'object', properties: {} } } }))
  if (json) body.response_format = { type: 'json_object' }
  const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' }
  if (a.apiKey) { headers.Authorization = `Bearer ${a.apiKey}`; headers['api-key'] = a.apiKey }
  let r: Response
  try { r = await fetch(a.endpoint, { method: 'POST', headers, body: JSON.stringify(body), signal: o.signal }) }
  catch (e: any) { throw new AiError(e?.name === 'AbortError' ? 'cancelled' : 'http', e?.message ?? 'Network error') }
  if (r.status === 429) throw new AiError('rate_limited', AI_MESSAGES.rate_limited)
  if (!r.ok) throw new AiError('http', `${r.status} ${r.statusText}`)
  const j = await r.json()
  const m = j?.choices?.[0]?.message
  if (!m) throw new AiError('http', 'Empty response')
  return m as Msg
}

export async function ask(input: string | { role: 'user' | 'assistant'; content: string }[], o: AskOptions = {}): Promise<string> {
  const messages: Msg[] = typeof input === 'string' ? [{ role: 'user', content: input }] : input.map((m) => ({ ...m }))
  for (let turn = 0; turn < 6; turn++) {
    const m = await complete(messages, o)
    if (m.tool_calls?.length && o.tools?.length) {
      messages.push({ role: 'assistant', content: m.content ?? null, tool_calls: m.tool_calls })
      for (const c of m.tool_calls) {
        const tool = o.tools.find((t) => t.name === c.function?.name)
        let out: unknown
        try { out = tool ? await tool.execute(JSON.parse(c.function?.arguments || '{}')) : { error: 'unknown tool' } } catch (e: any) { out = { error: e?.message ?? 'tool failed' } }
        messages.push({ role: 'tool', tool_call_id: c.id, content: JSON.stringify(out).slice(0, 60_000) })
      }
      continue
    }
    const text = m.content ?? ''
    o.onText?.(text)
    return text
  }
  throw new AiError('http', 'Too many tool steps')
}

export async function askJSON<T>(prompt: string, o: Omit<AskOptions, 'onText'> = {}): Promise<T> {
  const m = await complete([{ role: 'system', content: 'Reply with a single JSON object only, no prose.' }, { role: 'user', content: prompt }], o, true)
  const raw = (m.content ?? '').replace(/^```(?:json)?\s*|\s*```$/g, '')
  try { return JSON.parse(raw) as T } catch { throw new AiError('http', 'The AI reply was not valid JSON') }
}

/** true when an administrator has turned AI assist on and configured an endpoint. */
export function useAiAvailable(): boolean {
  return useStore((s) => !!(s.settings.ai?.enabled && s.settings.ai.endpoint))
}

===== FILE: src/core/auth/index.ts =====
// Login through a swappable OIDC interface (AGENT_RULES rule 20). The app uses only the user's ID, name and
// group claims; groups map to Crosswalk roles. The demo uses the persona switcher (no login, fictional people).
// A production OIDC provider (e.g. the company identity provider) implements the same interface — not built yet.
import type { Person, RoleId } from '../types'

export interface AuthUser { id: string; name: string; groups: string[] }

export interface AuthProvider {
  readonly kind: 'demo' | 'oidc'
  /** The signed-in user, or null when nobody is signed in. */
  currentUser(): AuthUser | null
  signIn(): Promise<void>
  signOut(): Promise<void>
}

/** Group claim → Crosswalk roles. Configured per deployment; the demo maps `crosswalk-<role>` to `<role>`. */
export type GroupRoleMap = Record<string, RoleId[]>

export function rolesForGroups(map: GroupRoleMap, groups: string[]): RoleId[] {
  return Array.from(new Set(groups.flatMap((g) => map[g] ?? [])))
}

export const demoGroupMap = (roles: RoleId[]): GroupRoleMap => Object.fromEntries(roles.map((r) => [`crosswalk-${r}`, [r]]))

/** Demo provider: the persona switcher picks one of the fictional people; their roles become group claims. */
export function demoAuthProvider(getPerson: () => Person | undefined): AuthProvider {
  return {
    kind: 'demo',
    currentUser() {
      const p = getPerson()
      return p ? { id: p.id, name: p.name, groups: p.roles.map((r) => `crosswalk-${r}`) } : null
    },
    async signIn() { /* demo: nothing to do */ },
    async signOut() { /* demo: nothing to do */ },
  }
}

===== FILE: src/core/data/index.ts =====
// The one data-access instance the app uses (rule 17): the shared Postgres-backed API. Swap the
// implementation here without touching any screen or the engine.
import { sharedDataAccess } from './server'
import type { DataAccess } from './types'

export type { DataAccess, RemoteChange, RemoteState, CommitResult } from './types'
export { gzipBase64, gunzipBase64 } from './server'
export const data: DataAccess = sharedDataAccess()

/** UI preferences through the data-access interface (kept in this browser only). */
export const prefs = {
  getRaw: (key: string) => data.getPref(key),
  setRaw: (key: string, value: string) => data.setPref(key, value),
  remove: (key: string) => data.removePref(key),
  get<T>(key: string, fallback: T): T {
    const v = data.getPref(key)
    if (v == null) return fallback
    try { return JSON.parse(v) as T } catch { return fallback }
  },
  set(key: string, value: unknown) { data.setPref(key, JSON.stringify(value)) },
}

===== FILE: src/core/data/server.ts =====
// Shared implementation of the data-access interface: the app's own API (src/server/api.ts) over relative
// URLs, so it works wherever the page is served from. The only network calls this app makes for data.
import type { CommitResult, DataAccess, RemoteChange } from './types'

function storage(): Storage | null {
  try { return (globalThis as { localStorage?: Storage }).localStorage ?? null } catch { return null }
}

async function call(path: string, init: RequestInit = {}, passcode?: string): Promise<Response | null> {
  try {
    const headers: Record<string, string> = { 'content-type': 'application/json' }
    if (passcode) headers['x-crosswalk-passcode'] = passcode
    return await fetch('api/' + path, { ...init, headers, cache: 'no-store' })
  } catch { return null }
}
const ok = async <T,>(r: Response | null): Promise<T | null> => (r && r.ok ? ((await r.json()) as T) : null)

export function sharedDataAccess(): DataAccess {
  const mem = new Map<string, string>()
  return {
    kind: 'shared',
    async health() {
      const r = await ok<{ ok: boolean; editable: boolean }>(await call('health'))
      return r?.ok ? { editable: !!r.editable } : null
    },
    async auth(passcode) {
      const r = await call('auth', { method: 'POST', body: '{}' }, passcode)
      return !!r && r.status === 204
    },
    async loadState(schemaVersion) {
      const r = await ok<{ generation: { id: number } | null; snapshot: { seq: number; stateGz: string } | null; changes: RemoteChange[]; head: number }>(await call(`state?schema=${schemaVersion}`))
      if (!r) throw new Error('Could not load the shared data')
      return { generation: r.generation?.id ?? null, snapshotGz: r.snapshot?.stateGz ?? null, snapshotSeq: r.snapshot?.seq ?? 0, changes: r.changes, head: r.head }
    },
    async changesSince(schemaVersion, generation, after) {
      const r = await ok<{ reset?: boolean; changes?: RemoteChange[]; head?: number }>(await call(`changes?schema=${schemaVersion}&generation=${generation}&after=${after}`))
      if (!r) throw new Error('Could not reach the shared data')
      return r.reset ? { reset: true } : { reset: false, changes: r.changes ?? [], head: r.head ?? after }
    },
    async appendChange(passcode, c): Promise<CommitResult> {
      const r = await call('changes', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify(c) }, passcode)
      if (!r) return 'error'
      if (r.ok) return 'ok'
      if (r.status === 409) return 'conflict'
      if (r.status === 401 || r.status === 403) return 'unauthorized'
      return 'error'
    },
    async addSnapshot(passcode, generation, seq, stateGz) {
      const r = await call('snapshot', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ generation, seq, stateGz }) }, passcode)
      return !!r && r.ok
    },
    async startGeneration(passcode, schema, reason, actor, stateGz) {
      const r = await ok<{ generation: { id: number } }>(await call('generation', { method: 'POST', body: JSON.stringify({ schema, reason, actor, stateGz }) }, passcode))
      return r?.generation.id ?? null
    },
    getPref(key) {
      try { const v = storage()?.getItem(key); if (v != null) return v } catch { /* blocked */ }
      return mem.get(key) ?? null
    },
    setPref(key, value) {
      mem.set(key, value)
      try { storage()?.setItem(key, value) } catch { /* blocked */ }
    },
    removePref(key) {
      mem.delete(key)
      try { storage()?.removeItem(key) } catch { /* blocked */ }
    },
  }
}

/** gzip + base64, so a ~3.5 MB state travels and is stored as a few hundred KB of text. */
export async function gzipBase64(text: string): Promise<string> {
  const stream = new Blob([text]).stream().pipeThrough(new CompressionStream('gzip'))
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(await new Response(stream).arrayBuffer())
  let bin = ''
  for (let i = 0; i < bytes.length; i += 0x8000) bin += String.fromCharCode(...bytes.subarray(i, i + 0x8000))
  return btoa(bin)
}
export async function gunzipBase64(b64: string): Promise<string> {
  const bin = atob(b64)
  const bytes = new Uint8Array(bin.length)
  for (let i = 0; i < bin.length; i++) bytes[i] = bin.charCodeAt(i)
  const stream = new Blob([bytes]).stream().pipeThrough(new DecompressionStream('gzip'))
  return await new Response(stream).text()
}

===== FILE: src/core/data/types.ts =====
// Data-access interface (AGENT_RULES rule 17). ALL persistence goes through an implementation of this
// interface; nothing else in the app touches storage or the network for data.
//
// The shared implementation (./server.ts) talks to the app's own API, which keeps the data in Postgres:
// a starting snapshot plus an append-only log of changes. When no API answers (the standalone HTML file,
// a static host, local dev without the API), the app runs in memory and says so; nothing is saved.
//
// Only small UI preferences (last tab, the persona you are acting as, the edit passcode) stay in the browser.

export interface RemoteChange { seq: number; actor: string; summary: string; patches: string }
export interface RemoteState { generation: number | null; snapshotGz: string | null; snapshotSeq: number; changes: RemoteChange[]; head: number }
export type CommitResult = 'ok' | 'conflict' | 'unauthorized' | 'error'

export interface DataAccess {
  readonly kind: 'shared'
  /** null when no API is reachable. */
  health(): Promise<{ editable: boolean } | null>
  /** True when the passcode is accepted for edits. */
  auth(passcode: string): Promise<boolean>
  loadState(schemaVersion: number): Promise<RemoteState>
  changesSince(schemaVersion: number, generation: number, after: number): Promise<{ reset: true } | { reset: false; changes: RemoteChange[]; head: number }>
  appendChange(passcode: string, c: { generation: number; seq: number; actor: string; summary: string; patches: unknown[] }): Promise<CommitResult>
  addSnapshot(passcode: string, generation: number, seq: number, stateGz: string): Promise<boolean>
  /** Start a new generation from a full snapshot (first edit, or "Reset demo data"). Returns its id. */
  startGeneration(passcode: string, schemaVersion: number, reason: string, actor: string, stateGz: string): Promise<number | null>
  /** Small per-user UI preferences (last tab, filters, passcode). Raw string values, kept in this browser. */
  getPref(key: string): string | null
  setPref(key: string, value: string): void
  removePref(key: string): void
}

===== FILE: src/core/download.ts =====
// Offer a generated file to the viewer as a normal browser download.
export async function saveFile(filename: string, data: string | Blob, mime = 'text/plain'): Promise<'saved' | 'declined' | 'unavailable'> {
  const blob = typeof data === 'string' ? new Blob([data], { type: mime }) : data
  const a = document.createElement('a')
  a.href = URL.createObjectURL(blob)
  a.download = filename
  a.click()
  setTimeout(() => URL.revokeObjectURL(a.href), 2000)
  return 'saved'
}

/** Rows → CSV text. */
export function toCSV(rows: Record<string, unknown>[]): string {
  if (!rows.length) return ''
  const cols = Object.keys(rows[0])
  const esc = (v: unknown) => { const s = v == null ? '' : String(v); return /[",\n]/.test(s) ? '"' + s.replace(/"/g, '""') + '"' : s }
  return [cols.join(','), ...rows.map((r) => cols.map((c) => esc(r[c])).join(','))].join('\n')
}

/** A standalone, printable HTML document (for SOPs, gate packets, evidence binders). */
export function htmlDoc(title: string, body: string): string {
  return `<!doctype html><html><head><meta charset="utf-8"><title>${title}</title><style>
  body{font-family:'Public Sans',system-ui,sans-serif;color:#141b24;max-width:900px;margin:40px auto;padding:0 24px;font-size:13px;line-height:1.5}
  h1{font-size:24px;margin:0 0 4px}h2{font-size:16px;border-bottom:2px solid #141b24;padding-bottom:4px;margin-top:28px}h3{font-size:14px}
  table{border-collapse:collapse;width:100%;margin:8px 0}th,td{border:1px solid #d6dce3;padding:5px 8px;text-align:left;vertical-align:top}th{background:#e9ecf0;font-size:11px;text-transform:uppercase;letter-spacing:.05em}
  td.num{text-align:right;font-variant-numeric:tabular-nums}.meta{color:#4f5a69}.tag{display:inline-block;border:1px solid #999;border-radius:3px;padding:0 4px;font-size:11px}
  @media print{body{margin:0}}</style></head><body>${body}</body></html>`
}

===== FILE: src/core/sync.ts =====
// Keeps this browser's copy of the data in step with the shared database (through the data-access interface).
//
// - Load: the latest snapshot plus every change after it, replayed with immer.
// - Edit: each local change goes to the server as immer patches, numbered in order. If someone else saved
//   first (409), we undo our pending changes, apply theirs, re-run ours on top and try again.
// - Others' edits: polled every few seconds while the page is visible.
// - No passcode: changes stay on this page only and the app says so. No API: the app runs in memory.
// Only the persona you are acting as (currentUserId) is per browser; everything else is shared.
import { applyPatches, produceWithPatches, type Patch } from 'immer'
import type { AppState } from './types'
import type { DataAccess } from './data/types'
import { buildSeed, SCHEMA_VERSION } from './seed'
import { applyChange, onLocalChange, onReset, replaceState, setSync, strip, useStore, USER_KEY, type LocalChange } from './store'

export const PASSCODE_KEY = 'crosswalk:passcode'
const LOCAL_KEYS = new Set(['currentUserId'])
const SNAPSHOT_EVERY = 50
const POLL_MS = 5000

/** Fields that are secrets (the AI gateway key, map-service tokens): never sent to the shared database. */
const SECRET_KEYS = new Set(['apiKey', 'token'])
/** A deep copy without secret fields. */
export function scrub<T>(v: T): T {
  if (Array.isArray(v)) return v.map(scrub) as T
  if (v && typeof v === 'object') return Object.fromEntries(Object.entries(v).filter(([k]) => !SECRET_KEYS.has(k)).map(([k, x]) => [k, scrub(x)])) as T
  return v
}
/** Patches that belong in the shared database: not per-browser fields, not secrets. */
const shared = (ps: Patch[]) => ps
  .filter((p) => !LOCAL_KEYS.has(String(p.path[0])) && !p.path.some((k) => SECRET_KEYS.has(String(k))))
  .map((p) => ('value' in p ? { ...p, value: scrub(p.value) } : p))

export interface SyncDeps {
  data: DataAccess
  gzip: (s: string) => Promise<string>
  gunzip: (s: string) => Promise<string>
  schema?: number
  /** Called when someone else's edits or a reset arrive (for a toast). */
  notify?: (msg: string) => void
}

export function createSync(deps: SyncDeps) {
  const schema = deps.schema ?? SCHEMA_VERSION
  let generation: number | null = null
  let seq = 0
  let passcode = ''
  let canEdit = false
  let queue: LocalChange[] = []
  let flushing = false
  let timer: ReturnType<typeof setInterval> | undefined

  const current = () => strip(useStore.getState())
  const localUser = (s: AppState) => {
    const want = deps.data.getPref(USER_KEY) ?? useStore.getState().currentUserId
    return s.people.some((p) => p.id === want) ? want : s.currentUserId
  }
  const withLocal = (s: AppState): AppState => ({ ...s, currentUserId: localUser(s) })
  const actor = () => useStore.getState().currentUserId
  /** The shared state as of `seq`: the current state with our unsent changes undone. */
  const baseState = () => queue.reduceRight<AppState>((s, c) => applyPatches(s, c.inverse), current())

  async function load(): Promise<void> {
    const r = await deps.data.loadState(schema)
    let s: AppState = r.snapshotGz ? JSON.parse(await deps.gunzip(r.snapshotGz)) : buildSeed()
    for (const c of r.changes) s = applyPatches(s, JSON.parse(c.patches))
    generation = r.generation
    seq = r.head
    queue = []
    replaceState(withLocal(s))
    setSync({ mode: 'shared', unsaved: false, error: null, saving: false })
  }

  async function startGeneration(reason: string, state: AppState): Promise<boolean> {
    const gz = await deps.gzip(JSON.stringify(scrub(state)))
    const id = await deps.data.startGeneration(passcode, schema, reason, actor(), gz)
    if (id == null) return false
    generation = id
    seq = 0
    return true
  }

  async function snapshot() {
    if (generation == null) return
    const g = generation, at = seq
    const gz = await deps.gzip(JSON.stringify(scrub(baseState())))
    await deps.data.addSnapshot(passcode, g, at, gz)
  }

  /** Undo pending changes, apply others' changes, re-run ours on top. */
  async function rebase(): Promise<'ok' | 'reset'> {
    const r = await deps.data.changesSince(schema, generation!, seq)
    if (r.reset) return 'reset'
    let s = baseState()
    for (const c of r.changes) s = applyPatches(s, JSON.parse(c.patches))
    seq = r.head
    const redone: LocalChange[] = []
    for (const c of queue) {
      try {
        const [next, patches, inverse] = produceWithPatches(s, (d: AppState) => applyChange(d, c.fn, c.audit))
        s = next
        if (shared(patches).length) redone.push({ ...c, patches: shared(patches), inverse: shared(inverse) })
      } catch { deps.notify?.('One of your changes no longer applies after someone else’s edit and was dropped.') }
    }
    queue = redone
    replaceState(s)
    return 'ok'
  }

  async function flush(): Promise<void> {
    if (flushing) return
    flushing = true
    setSync({ saving: true })
    try {
      while (queue.length) {
        if (generation == null && !(await startGeneration('First edit', baseState()))) {
          setSync({ error: 'Couldn’t save. Retrying…' }); await wait(4000); continue
        }
        const c = queue[0]
        const res = await deps.data.appendChange(passcode, { generation: generation!, seq: seq + 1, actor: actor(), summary: c.audit?.summary ?? '', patches: c.patches })
        if (res === 'ok') {
          seq += 1
          queue.shift()
          setSync({ error: null, lastSavedAt: new Date().toISOString() })
          if (seq % SNAPSHOT_EVERY === 0) void snapshot()
        } else if (res === 'conflict') {
          if ((await rebase()) === 'reset') { deps.notify?.('The demo data was reset by someone else. Your unsaved change was dropped.'); await load(); break }
        } else if (res === 'unauthorized') {
          lock()
          deps.notify?.('The edit passcode was changed. Enter the new one to keep editing.')
          await load()
          break
        } else {
          setSync({ error: 'Couldn’t save. Retrying…' })
          await wait(4000)
        }
      }
    } finally {
      flushing = false
      setSync({ saving: false })
    }
  }

  function onChange(c: LocalChange) {
    const patches = shared(c.patches)
    if (!patches.length) return
    const mode = useStore.getState().sync.mode
    if (mode !== 'shared') return // offline: memory only, the banner says so
    if (!canEdit) { if (!c.background) setSync({ unsaved: true }); return }
    queue.push({ ...c, patches, inverse: shared(c.inverse) })
    void flush()
  }

  async function poll(): Promise<void> {
    if (flushing || queue.length || useStore.getState().sync.mode !== 'shared') return
    if (typeof document !== 'undefined' && document.visibilityState === 'hidden') return
    if (useStore.getState().sync.unsaved) return // keep the viewer's local changes until they reload or unlock
    try {
      if (generation == null) {
        const r = await deps.data.loadState(schema)
        if (r.generation != null) await load()
        return
      }
      const r = await deps.data.changesSince(schema, generation, seq)
      if (flushing || queue.length) return
      if (r.reset) { await load(); deps.notify?.('The demo data was reset.'); return }
      if (!r.changes.length) return
      let s = current()
      for (const c of r.changes) s = applyPatches(s, JSON.parse(c.patches))
      seq = r.head
      replaceState(withLocal(s))
      setSync({ error: null })
    } catch { setSync({ error: 'Can’t reach the shared data. Retrying…' }) }
  }

  function lock() {
    passcode = ''
    canEdit = false
    queue = []
    deps.data.removePref(PASSCODE_KEY)
    setSync({ canEdit: false })
  }

  async function resetShared() {
    const seed = buildSeed()
    if (!canEdit) { replaceState(withLocal(seed)); setSync({ unsaved: false }); return }
    queue = []
    if (await startGeneration('Reset demo data', seed)) { replaceState(withLocal(seed)); setSync({ unsaved: false }) }
    else deps.notify?.('Couldn’t reset the shared data. Try again.')
  }

  return {
    /** Connect to the API, load the shared data and start listening. Falls back to memory when no API answers. */
    async start(opts: { poll?: boolean } = {}) {
      setSync({ mode: 'connecting' })
      const h = await deps.data.health()
      if (!h) { replaceState(withLocal(current())); setSync({ mode: 'offline' }); return }
      const saved = deps.data.getPref(PASSCODE_KEY) ?? ''
      canEdit = h.editable && !!saved && (await deps.data.auth(saved))
      passcode = canEdit ? saved : ''
      if (saved && !canEdit) deps.data.removePref(PASSCODE_KEY)
      try { await load() } catch { setSync({ mode: 'offline' }); return }
      setSync({ editable: h.editable, canEdit })
      onLocalChange(onChange)
      onReset(() => { void resetShared() })
      if (opts.poll !== false && typeof window !== 'undefined') timer = setInterval(() => { void poll() }, POLL_MS)
    },
    /** Check the passcode; on success reload the shared data (dropping view-only changes) and allow edits. */
    async unlock(code: string): Promise<boolean> {
      if (!(await deps.data.auth(code))) return false
      passcode = code
      canEdit = true
      deps.data.setPref(PASSCODE_KEY, code)
      await load()
      setSync({ canEdit: true })
      return true
    },
    lock,
    /** Throw away view-only changes and show the shared data again. */
    async discardLocal() { await load() },
    poll,
    flush,
    /** Resolves once every local change has been saved (or after `ms`). */
    async idle(ms = 10000) {
      const until = Date.now() + ms
      while ((flushing || queue.length) && Date.now() < until) await wait(10)
      return !flushing && !queue.length
    },
    stop() { if (timer) clearInterval(timer); onLocalChange(null); onReset(null) },
    /** For tests. */
    get position() { return { generation, seq, pending: queue.length } },
  }
}
export type Sync = ReturnType<typeof createSync>

const wait = (ms: number) => new Promise((r) => setTimeout(r, ms))

===== FILE: src/core/syncClient.ts =====
// The app's one sync instance (started by App on load).
import { data, gunzipBase64, gzipBase64 } from './data'
import { createSync, type Sync } from './sync'

export const appSync: { current: Sync | null } = { current: null }

export function startAppSync(notify: (msg: string) => void): Sync {
  appSync.current?.stop()
  const s = createSync({ data, gzip: gzipBase64, gunzip: gunzipBase64, notify })
  appSync.current = s
  return s
}

===== FILE: src/core/seed/factorModel.ts =====
// Seed data for the Class 5 factor model: settings, three model versions (retired, published, draft), cost drivers for
// every demo project, and ~60 fictional closed projects for the backtest. The backtest set is generated from a hidden
// "true" cost process plus noise, so the published model is close but not exact — like a real fit.
// All values are fictional ("Arroyo Valley Gas").
import type { AppState, AssetType, AuditEntry, BacktestProject, DiameterBand, DriverValues, FactorModel, FactorModelSettings, HistoricalCost, HistUnit, ModelBaseRate, ModelFactor, ProjectDrivers, Terrain, WorkMethod } from '../types'
import { inputsOfRecord, priceFactor } from '../engine/factorModel'
import { rng } from '../util'

export const FACTOR_SETTINGS: FactorModelSettings = {
  overrideReasons: [
    'Site condition the drivers do not capture',
    'Scope not represented in the model population',
    'Known market or vendor pricing',
    'Quantity or scope correction from the requester',
    'Constructability review finding',
    'Outage window or schedule constraint',
  ],
  // VERIFY: publish-gate thresholds (MAPE, bias, P10–P90 coverage) are demo defaults set by the estimating lead.
  gate: { maxMape: 25, minCoverage: 70, maxCoverage: 95, maxAbsBias: 10 },
  iterations: 5000,
}

const LINEAR: AssetType[] = ['transmission-pipeline', 'distribution-main']
const L = (value: string, label: string, multiplier: number) => ({ value, label, multiplier })

// VERIFY: default factor multipliers, weights and cv are illustrative planning values for the fictional utility,
// to be replaced by the estimating team's own calibration.
export const DEFAULT_FACTORS: ModelFactor[] = [

  { id: 'f-cross', name: 'Crossings', kind: 'physical', driver: 'crossings', weight: 1, cv: 0.15, appliesTo: LINEAR, levels: [L('0', 'None', 0.96), L('1-2', '1–2', 1), L('3-5', '3–5', 1.08), L('6+', '6 or more', 1.18)], note: 'Bored or drilled road, rail and water crossings' },
  { id: 'f-setting', name: 'Setting', kind: 'physical', driver: 'terrain', weight: 1, cv: 0.08, appliesTo: LINEAR, levels: [L('urban', 'Urban', 1.22), L('suburban', 'Suburban', 1), L('rural', 'Rural', 0.75)], note: 'Restoration, substructures and congestion' },
  { id: 'f-permit', name: 'Permit complexity', kind: 'legal', driver: 'permitComplexity', weight: 1, cv: 0.15, appliesTo: [...LINEAR, 'valve-site', 'regulator-station'], levels: [L('ministerial', 'Ministerial', 1), L('discretionary', 'Discretionary', 1.08), L('major', 'Major', 1.2)], note: 'Agency review, CEQA documents, conditions of approval. Compressor stations: air permits and CEQA are already in the base rate' },
  { id: 'f-hca', name: 'High consequence area', kind: 'legal', driver: 'hca', weight: 1, cv: 0.05, appliesTo: ['transmission-pipeline', 'valve-site'], levels: [L('no', 'Not HCA', 0.96), L('yes', 'HCA', 1)], note: 'Integrity-management inspection and records' },
  { id: 'f-class', name: 'Class location', kind: 'legal', driver: 'classLocation', weight: 0.6, cv: 0.05, appliesTo: ['transmission-pipeline'], levels: [L('1', 'Class 1', 0.97), L('2', 'Class 2', 1), L('3', 'Class 3', 1.06), L('4', 'Class 4', 1.1)], note: 'Design factor and valve spacing. Weighted below 1: partly captured by setting' },

Weighted below 1: partly captured by setting' },
  { id: 'f-hours', name: 'Work-hour restrictions', kind: 'logistical', driver: 'workHours', weight: 1, cv: 0.12, appliesTo: [...LINEAR, 'valve-site', 'regulator-station'], levels: [L('standard', 'Standard', 1), L('restricted', 'Restricted hours', 1.08), L('night', 'Night work', 1.2)], note: 'Shortened shifts, night premiums, lighting' },
  { id: 'f-remote', name: 'Remoteness / access', kind: 'logistical', driver: 'remoteness', weight: 1, cv: 0.1, levels: [L('local', 'Local', 1), L('regional', 'Regional', 1.04), L('remote', 'Remote', 1.12)], note: 'Haul, crew travel and per diem' },
]

/** Reference quantity each historical rate was observed at (the representative project size). */
const REF_QTY = (a: AssetType, wm: WorkMethod): number =>
  a === 'transmission-pipeline' ? (wm === 'hydrotest' ? 25_000 : 15_000) : a === 'distribution-main' ? 8_000 : a === 'valve-site' ? 3 : a === 'compressor-station' ? (wm === 'compressor-replacement' ? 22_000 : 9_000) : 1

/** Base rates from the historical cost table: the suburban (or all-settings) P50 for each asset × method × band. */
export function baseRatesFromHistory(hist: HistoricalCost[]): ModelBaseRate[] {
  const groups = new Map<string, HistoricalCost[]>()
  for (const h of hist.filter((x) => x.active)) {
    const k = `${h.assetType}|${h.workMethod}|${h.diameterBand}`
    groups.set(k, [...(groups.get(k) ?? []), h])
  }
  return Array.from(groups.values()).map((rows, k) => {
    const h = rows.find((x) => x.terrain === 'suburban') ?? rows.find((x) => x.terrain === 'any') ?? rows[0]
    return { id: `br-${k + 1}`, assetType: h.assetType, workMethod: h.workMethod, diameterBand: h.diameterBand, unit: h.unit, rate: h.p50, refQty: REF_QTY(h.assetType, h.workMethod), baseYear: h.baseYear, n: rows.reduce((a, x) => a + x.n, 0), mix: { ...h.mix }, sourceRowId: h.id }
  })
}

const clone = <T,>(x: T): T => JSON.parse(JSON.stringify(x))
const setMult = (fs: ModelFactor[], id: string, value: string, m: number) => { const l = fs.find((f) => f.id === id)?.levels.find((x) => x.value === value); if (l) l.multiplier = m }
const setWeight = (fs: ModelFactor[], id: string, w: number) => { const f = fs.find((x) => x.id === id); if (f) f.weight = w }

/** v1 (retired), v2 (published, the model of record), v3 (an estimator's draft refit). */
export function buildFactorModels(hist: HistoricalCost[]): FactorModel[] {
  const base = baseRatesFromHistory(hist)
  const f1 = clone(DEFAULT_FACTORS).filter((f) => f.id !== 'f-class')
  setWeight(f1, 'f-cross', 0.5); setWeight(f1, 'f-traffic', 1); setMult(f1, 'f-soil', 'rock', 1.25)
  const b3 = clone(base)
  for (const r of b3) if (r.assetType === 'transmission-pipeline' && r.workMethod === 'replace-open-trench') r.rate = Math.round(r.rate * 0.95)
  const f3 = clone(DEFAULT_FACTORS)
  setMult(f3, 'f-soil', 'rock', 1.42); setMult(f3, 'f-soil', 'cobble', 1.17); setWeight(f3, 'f-traffic', 0.9); setMult(f3, 'f-cross', '6+', 1.21)
  return [
    { id: 'fm-1', name: 'Class 5 factor model', version: 1, status: 'retired', baseRates: clone(base), scaleExponent: 1, baseCv: 0.3, factors: f1, notes: 'First model: linear in quantity, crossings at half weight.', createdById: 'u-est1', createdAt: '2025-02-03T17:00:00.000Z', approvedById: 'u-estl', approvedAt: '2025-02-20T17:00:00.000Z', retiredAt: '2026-03-16T17:00:00.000Z', backtest: { at: '2025-02-10T17:00:00.000Z', n: 44, mape: 24.1, medianApe: 19.8, bias: 6.2, coverage: 88.6, passed: true } },
    { id: 'fm-2', name: 'Class 5 factor model', version: 2, status: 'published', baseRates: clone(base), scaleExponent: 0.92, baseCv: 0.15, factors: clone(DEFAULT_FACTORS), notes: 'Size scale fitted (0.92); class location added at 0.6 weight; traffic reduced to 0.8 weight where setting already explains it.', createdById: 'u-est1', createdAt: '2026-02-24T17:00:00.000Z', parentVersion: 1, submittedAt: '2026-03-02T17:00:00.000Z', approvedById: 'u-estl', approvedAt: '2026-03-16T17:00:00.000Z' },
    { id: 'fm-3', name: 'Class 5 factor model', version: 3, status: 'draft', baseRates: b3, scaleExponent: 0.9, baseCv: 0.15, factors: f3, notes: 'Refit after the 2026 closeouts: rock and cobble up, open-trench base rates down 5%, size scale 0.92 → 0.90, traffic weight back to 0.9.', createdById: 'u-est2', createdAt: '2026-10-19T17:00:00.000Z', parentVersion: 2 },
  ]
}

/** Audit history for the seeded versions (append-only log). */
export function factorModelAudit(models: FactorModel[]): AuditEntry[] {
  const a = (at: string, actorId: string, entityId: string, action: string, summary: string, k: number): AuditEntry => ({ id: `aud-fm-${k}`, at, actorId, entity: 'factor-model', entityId, action, summary })
  const v2 = models.find((m) => m.id === 'fm-2')!, v3 = models.find((m) => m.id === 'fm-3')!
  return [
    a('2025-02-03T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-est1', 'fm-1', 'create', 'Factor model v1 created from the historical cost table', 1),
    a('2025-02-20T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-estl', 'fm-1', 'publish', 'Factor model v1 approved and published', 2),
    a('2026-02-24T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-est1', 'fm-2', 'create', 'Factor model v2 drafted from v1', 3),
    a('2026-02-26T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-est1', 'fm-2', 'update', 'Factor model v2 draft: Scale exponent 1.00 → 0.92; Base uncertainty cv 0.30 → 0.15; Added factor Class location (Class location, weight 0.60); Crossings: weight 0.50 → 1.00; Traffic: weight 1.00 → 0.80; Soil / rock · Rock: ×1.25 → ×1.35', 4),
    a(v2.submittedAt!, 'u-est1', 'fm-2', 'submit', 'Factor model v2 submitted for approval', 5),
    a('2026-03-16T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-estl', 'fm-1', 'retire', 'Factor model v1 retired — replaced by v2', 6),
    a('2026-03-16T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-estl', 'fm-2', 'publish', 'Factor model v2 approved and published', 7),
    a(v3.createdAt, 'u-est2', 'fm-3', 'create', 'Factor model v3 drafted from v2', 8),
    a('2026-10-21T17:00:00.000Z', 'u-est2', 'fm-3', 'update', 'Factor model v3 draft: Scale exponent 0.92 → 0.90; Base rates, transmission open-trench replacement (all bands): −5%; Soil / rock · Cobble / caliche: ×1.15 → ×1.17; Soil / rock · Rock: ×1.35 → ×1.42; Crossings · 6 or more: ×1.18 → ×1.21; Traffic: weight 0.80 → 0.90', 9),
  ]
}

// ───────────────────────────── Drivers
type R = ReturnType<typeof rng>
function driversFor(R: R, asset: AssetType, wm: WorkMethod, terrain: Terrain, lengthFt: number): ProjectDrivers {
  const linear = LINEAR.includes(asset)
  const station = asset === 'compressor-station' || asset === 'regulator-station'
  return {
    soil: terrain === 'rural' ? R.pick(['normal', 'cobble', 'cobble', 'rock'] as const) : R.pick(['normal', 'normal', 'normal', 'normal', 'cobble', 'rock'] as const),
    permitComplexity: asset === 'compressor-station' ? 'major' : terrain === 'urban' ? R.pick(['ministerial', 'discretionary', 'discretionary'] as const) : terrain === 'rural' ? R.pick(['ministerial', 'ministerial', 'discretionary', 'major'] as const) : R.pick(['ministerial', 'ministerial', 'ministerial', 'discretionary'] as const),
    traffic: station ? 'low' : terrain === 'urban' ? R.pick(['heavy', 'heavy', 'moderate'] as const) : terrain === 'suburban' ? R.pick(['moderate', 'moderate', 'low'] as const) : 'low',
    workHours: station ? 'standard' : terrain === 'urban' ? R.pick(['standard', 'restricted', 'restricted', 'night'] as const) : terrain === 'suburban' ? R.pick(['standard', 'standard', 'restricted'] as const) : 'standard',
    crossings: !linear ? 0 : wm === 'replace-hdd' ? R.int(1, 4) : wm === 'hydrotest' ? R.int(0, 2) : Math.min(9, Math.round(lengthFt / 6000) + R.int(0, 2)),
    remoteness: terrain === 'rural' ? R.pick(['local', 'regional', 'regional', 'remote'] as const) : terrain === 'suburban' ? R.pick(['local', 'local', 'regional'] as const) : 'local',
  }
}

/** Record plausible fictional drivers on every demo project (deterministic). Mutates the state. */
export function seedProjectDrivers(s: AppState) {
  const R = rng(3131)
  for (const p of s.projects) p.drivers = driversFor(R, p.asset.type, p.workMethod, p.location.terrain, p.asset.lengthFt)
}

// ───────────────────────────── Backtest set
const PLACES = ['Cedar Hollow', 'Mesa Alta', 'Quail Run', 'Agua Fria', 'Los Robles', 'Copper Flat', 'Sunset Bluff', 'Pinecrest', 'Rio Seco', 'Granite Pass', 'Oak Terrace', 'Harbor Point', 'Dry Creek', 'Willow Bend', 'Saddleback', 'Coyote Wash', 'Bayview', 'Lone Pine', 'Arroyo Verde', 'Brookfield']
const DISTRICT_COUNTY: Record<string, string> = { West: 'Coastline County', East: 'Arroyo County', South: 'Sierra Mesa County', North: 'Arroyo County' }

/**
 * The hidden "true" cost process the fictional history was generated from. It is NOT used by any estimate — only to make
 * the synthetic actuals. It differs from the published model on purpose (rock, size scale, crossings, permits, bias by asset).
 */
function trueModel(published: FactorModel): FactorModel {
  const m = clone(published)
  m.scaleExponent = 0.89
  setMult(m.factors, 'f-soil', 'rock', 1.45); setMult(m.factors, 'f-soil', 'cobble', 1.18)
  setMult(m.factors, 'f-setting', 'urban', 1.26); setMult(m.factors, 'f-setting', 'rural', 0.73)
  setMult(m.factors, 'f-cross', '3-5', 1.1); setMult(m.factors, 'f-cross', '6+', 1.23)
  setMult(m.factors, 'f-permit', 'major', 1.22); setMult(m.factors, 'f-hours', 'night', 1.23); setMult(m.factors, 'f-remote', 'remote', 1.14)
  setWeight(m.factors, 'f-traffic', 0.9)
  const bias: Partial<Record<string, number>> = { 'replace-hdd': 1.05, 'distribution-main': 0.97, 'compressor-station': 1.04, 'valve-site': 1.02 }
  for (const b of m.baseRates) b.rate *= (bias[b.workMethod] ?? 1) * (bias[b.assetType] ?? 1)
  return m
}

const MIX: { asset: AssetType; wm: WorkMethod; n: number; bands: DiameterBand[]; unit: HistUnit; kind: string }[] = [
  { asset: 'transmission-pipeline', wm: 'replace-open-trench', n: 16, bands: ['10-16', '20-24', '26-36'], unit: 'ft', kind: 'Replacement' },
  { asset: 'transmission-pipeline', wm: 'replace-hdd', n: 8, bands: ['10-16', '20-24', '26-36'], unit: 'ft', kind: 'HDD Replacement' },
  { asset: 'transmission-pipeline', wm: 'hydrotest', n: 10, bands: ['n/a'], unit: 'ft', kind: 'Hydrotest' },
  { asset: 'distribution-main', wm: 'replace-open-trench', n: 12, bands: ['le-8'], unit: 'ft', kind: 'Main Replacement' },
  { asset: 'valve-site', wm: 'valve-automation', n: 8, bands: ['n/a'], unit: 'site', kind: 'Valve Automation' },
  { asset: 'compressor-station', wm: 'compressor-replacement', n: 2, bands: ['n/a'], unit: 'hp', kind: 'Compressor Replacement' },
  { asset: 'compressor-station', wm: 'station-rebuild', n: 1, bands: ['n/a'], unit: 'hp', kind: 'Station Rebuild' },
  { asset: 'regulator-station', wm: 'station-rebuild', n: 3, bands: ['n/a'], unit: 'station', kind: 'Regulator Station Rebuild' },
]

/** ~60 fictional closed projects with drivers and actual cost, generated from the hidden process + noise. */
export function buildBacktestProjects(s: AppState): BacktestProject[] {
  const published = s.factorModels.find((m) => m.status === 'published')
  if (!published) return []
  const truth = trueModel(published)
  const R = rng(6161)
  const normal = () => { const u = Math.max(1e-12, R.next()), v = R.next(); return Math.sqrt(-2 * Math.log(u)) * Math.cos(2 * Math.PI * v) }
  const regionTrue: Record<string, number> = { West: 1.03, East: 1, South: 0.98, North: 1 }
  const taxOf = (district: string) => s.settings.estimating.taxRates.find((t) => t.county === DISTRICT_COUNTY[district])?.rate ?? 0
  const out: BacktestProject[] = []
  let k = 0
  for (const g of MIX) {
    for (let j = 0; j < g.n; j++) {
      k++
      const terrain: Terrain = g.asset === 'compressor-station' ? 'rural' : R.pick(['urban', 'suburban', 'suburban', 'rural'] as const)
      const district = R.pick(['North', 'West', 'South', 'East'] as const)
      const ref = REF_QTY(g.asset, g.wm)
      const qty = g.unit === 'site' ? R.int(1, 5) : g.unit === 'station' ? 1 : Math.round((ref * Math.exp(R.range(Math.log(0.3), Math.log(2.5)))) / (g.unit === 'hp' ? 500 : 100)) * (g.unit === 'hp' ? 500 : 100)
      const pd = driversFor(R, g.asset, g.wm, terrain, g.unit === 'ft' ? qty : 0)
      const drivers: DriverValues = { ...pd, terrain, classLocation: terrain === 'urban' ? R.pick([3, 3, 4] as const) : terrain === 'suburban' ? R.pick([2, 2, 3] as const) : R.pick([1, 1, 2] as const), hca: terrain !== 'rural' || R.chance(0.2) }
      const year = R.int(2023, 2026)
      const rec: BacktestProject = {
        id: `bt-${k}`, code: `CL-${year}-${String(k).padStart(3, '0')}`,
        name: `${g.asset === 'transmission-pipeline' ? `Line AV-${40 + k} ` : g.asset === 'distribution-main' ? 'Tract ' : ''}${g.kind} — ${PLACES[k % PLACES.length]}`,
        assetType: g.asset, workMethod: g.wm, diameterBand: R.pick(g.bands), unit: g.unit, qty, district, midpointYear: year, taxPct: taxOf(district), drivers, actual: 0,
      }
      // VERIFY: synthetic actuals = hidden process × lognormal noise (σ 0.20); fictional data for the demo backtest only.
      const cost = priceFactor(s, truth, inputsOfRecord(rec)).cost
      rec.actual = Math.round((cost * (regionTrue[district] ?? 1) * Math.exp(0.2 * normal())) / 1000) * 1000
      out.push(rec)
    }
  }
  return out
}

===== FILE: src/core/seed/index.ts =====
// Demo data for a fictional utility, "Arroyo Valley Gas". History is REPLAYED through the real
// engine (gates, estimates, workflows, snapshots) with a pinned clock, so every number ties out.
import type { AppState, AssetType, Estimate, EstimateLine, InternalOrder, Project, Risk, WorkMethod, Terrain, Milestone, SafetyPlan } from '../types'

export const SCHEMA_VERSION = 9
export const AS_OF = '2026-10-28'

type Spec = {
  n: number; name: string; prog: string; asset: AssetType; wm: WorkMethod; dia: number; len: number; terrain: Terrain; district: string; stage: number; closed?: boolean; hold?: boolean; sites?: number; city: string
}
const S = (n: number, name: string, prog: string, asset: AssetType, wm: WorkMethod, dia: number, len: number, terrain: Terrain, district: string, city: string, stage: number, extra: Partial<Spec> = {}): Spec =>
  ({ n, name, prog, asset, wm, dia, len, terrain, district, city, stage, ...extra })

const DISTRICT_BOX: Record<string, [number, number, number, number]> = {
  West: [90, 300, 330, 540], East: [360, 260, 620, 500], South: [650, 220, 930, 520], North: [260, 50, 720, 210],
}
const COUNTY: Record<string, string> = { West: 'Coastline County', East: 'Arroyo County', South: 'Sierra Mesa County', North: 'Arroyo County' }

const PM_BY_PROG: Record<string, string[]> = { 'prg-pse': ['u-pm1', 'u-pm2'], 'prg-tim': ['u-pm3', 'u-pm4'], 'prg-drp': ['u-pm1', 'u-pm3'], 'prg-csm': ['u-pm5', 'u-pm6'], 'prg-vap': ['u-pm4', 'u-pm2'] }
const SPONSOR_BY_PROG: Record<string, string> = { 'prg-pse': 'u-dir1', 'prg-tim': 'u-dir1', 'prg-drp': 'u-dir1', 'prg-csm': 'u-dir2', 'prg-vap': 'u-dir1' }
const PGM_BY_PROG: Record<string, string> = { 'prg-pse': 'u-pgm1', 'prg-tim': 'u-pgm2', 'prg-drp': 'u-pgm1', 'prg-csm': 'u-pgm3', 'prg-vap': 'u-pgm2' }

const MILESTONES_STD = ['Business case approved', 'Option selected', 'Construction authorized', 'In service', 'Closeout complete']
const MILESTONES_SMALL = ['Plan approved', 'Construction authorized', 'In service & closed']

        { key: 'pseSegment', label: 'Safety enhancement segment ID', programIds: ['prg-pse'], requiredAtStage: 1 },
